claims:
- kind: summary
  text: Tenderers must pay a non-refundable tender fee of INR 11,800/- inclusive of GST via online payment (RTGS/NEFT/IMPS)
    using specified bank details and upload the proof on the eProcurement portal.
  evidence_ids:
  - S22a916adcdb444fc
  - J36c62c71b3666400
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: Ce8e8a2578da4e4c8
  citations:
  - id: S22a916adcdb444fc
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA.md
    okf_sha256: 1d19d2821187107d8bead3c38cac38cb70cac496bf9b5b067362f9b891f92cfb
    heading: Instruction 2 — Tender Fee Payment
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    pdf_pages:
    - 8
    quote: '- **Non-Refundable Tender Fee:** Payable as stipulated in Key Details through online payment only, using
      RTGS / NEFT / IMPS.

      - **Payment Proof:** To be uploaded on the eProcurement Portal with the tender submission.

      - **Transaction Reference:** The Tender Reference Number must be mentioned in the payment reference or remarks.

      - **Beneficiary Name:** BANGALORE METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED.

      - **Bank Name:** BANK OF INDIA.

      - **Branch:** CANTONMENT BRANCH, BENGALURU.

      - **Account Number:** `840420110000760`.

      - **RTGS/IFSC:** `BKID0008404`.'
    page_basis: OKF reference, not independently PDF-verified
    page_precision: section_reference

      \ pay a non-refundable Tender fee as stipulated in Key Details through online\\n         payment only (using\
      \ RTGS/ NEFT/ IMPS). Proof of Tender fee paid through online shall be uploaded\\n         on eProcurement\
      \ Portal along with tender submission. Tenderer must mention the Tender\\n          Reference Number in the\
      \ reference/remarks at the time of the online transaction of payment.\\n\\n                     For online\
      \ payment following details are to be considered:\\n\\n                 Name: BANGALORE METRO RAIL CORPORATION\

- kind: summary
  text: Prospective tenderers may request site visits by submitting representative details to the Site Visit Coordinator
    at least one day in advance, no later than 11:00 Hrs on the preceding day.
  evidence_ids:
  - J0f81bcbd307215d6
  - S58526eb02b326b26
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C26d849764606b200
  citations:
  - id: J0f81bcbd307215d6
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e3cf38f3c94155bb807c3dc1374cc20588ab715e06455bf88cda54b8e92b1665
    heading: Clause 4.0 — Site Visit
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 11
    - 12
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/3
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"4.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"\
      4.0       SITE VISIT:\\n\\n  4.1     BMRCL will facilitate a site visit on the date and time specified in\
      \ the Notice Inviting Tender\\n              (NIT). Prospective tenderers must submit the details of their\
      \ representative(s)—a maximum\\n               of two—to the Site Visit Coordinator at the e-mail address\
      \ provided below, at least one day\\n                in advance. The submission deadline is 11:00 Hrs on the\
      \ day preceding the scheduled site\\n                   visit.\\n\\n            For further coordination in\
      \ connection with site visit, please contact the following BMRCL\\n                  official:\\n\\n     \
      \         Site Visit Coordinator:\\n\\n             Name: Mr. Ravindra H. Bettaller\\n\\n                \
      \ Designation: Additional Chief Engineer\\n\\n                Email (Official): ravindra@bmrc.co.in\\n\\n\
      \                Contact No (Mob): 8277890469\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"\
      (i)  Any site information provided in this Tender Document is for the Tenderer’s general\\n              \
      \        guidance only. The Tenderer shall be responsible for visiting and examining the Site of\\n      \
      \             Works and its surroundings at their own risk and cost, and for obtaining all information\\n\
      \                      necessary for the preparation of the Tender and the execution of the Contract.\\n\\\
      n                      Permission for the Tenderer to enter Employer’s premises and lands shall be deemed\
      \ to\\n                    have been granted on the express condition that the Tenderer, its personnel, and\
      \ agents\\n                           shall indemnify and hold harmless the Employer, its personnel, and agents\
      \ from and\\n                        against all claims, liabilities, costs, and expenses whatsoever, including\
      \ but not limited to,\\n                     any death, personal injury, or loss of or damage to property\
      \ arising out of or in connection\\n                         with such site visit.\\n\\n                 \
      \                 (ii) The Tenderer shall be deemed to have inspected the site, its surroundings, and all\\\
      n                         relevant conditions, including sources of materials, access roads, environmental\
      \ factors,\\n                    and time-related restrictions, and to have accepted full responsibility for\
      \ the implications\\n                          thereof. No claim whatsoever shall be entertained on the grounds\
      \ of lack of knowledge\\n                         or ignorance of such site conditions.\"\n    }\n  ],\n \
      \ \"heading_line\": \"4.0       SITE VISIT:\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
    heading_alignment: exact_number_match

    by the deadline mentioned in the NIT.
  evidence_ids:
  - J77602b0c2f9e03d2
  - Sa3f5c5a1eadc5278
  relation_type: none

  - id: J77602b0c2f9e03d2
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e3cf38f3c94155bb807c3dc1374cc20588ab715e06455bf88cda54b8e92b1665
    heading: Clause 6.0 — Clarification on Tender Documents
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 12
    - 13
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/5
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"6.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"\
      6.0      CLARIFICATION ON TENDER DOCUMENTS\\n\\n         6.1       While all efforts have been made to avoid\
      \ errors in drafting the Tender documents, the\\n                  Tenderer is advised to check the same carefully\
      \ and seek clarifications within the scheduled\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 13,\n      \"text\":\
      \ \"period only. No claim on account of any errors detected in the Tender documents later shall\\n       \
      \    be entertained.\\n\\n  6.2      The tenderer shall submit/upload any query on the eProcurement Portal\
      \ not later than the last\\n             date of seeking clarification as mentioned in NIT. Only the Clarifications\
      \ sought through the\\n               portal will be considered.\\n\\n  6.3      A Pre-Bid Meeting will be\
      \ held on the date and time specified in the NIT. The meeting will be\\n             accessible via video\
      \ conference. To receive the virtual meeting link and access credentials\\n                (e.g., software,\
      \ meeting ID, password), prospective bidders must register the details of their\\n              representative(s)\
      \ - a maximum of two - by sending the information to the official email of GM\\n              (Contracts),\
      \ BMRCL, at contracts@bmrc.co.in. Registration must be completed at least one\\n           day in advance,\
      \ no later than 17:00 Hrs on the day preceding the meeting.\\n\\n            Non-attendance at the Pre-bid\
      \ meeting will not be a cause for disqualification of a Tenderer.\\n\\n  6.4       Response in writing for\
      \ the Clarifications sought through the portal will be made available at\\n             the eProcurement Portal.\
      \ Bidders are advised to visit the Portal frequently.\\n\\n  6.5       Tenderers to note that seeking clarification\
      \ regarding qualification shall only be for criteria set\\n                for Eligibility and Qualification.\
      \ Bidder has to read the tender document completely and\\n            understand the requirement on his own\
      \ and employer will not advice any prospective tenderer\\n             regarding participation in this tender.\
      \ Employer reserves the right not to reply to presumptive\\n              or hypothetical queries.\"\n   \
      \ }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"6.0      CLARIFICATION ON TENDER DOCUMENTS\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
      \n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
    heading_alignment: exact_number_match

- kind: summary
  text: The technical bid must be uploaded under the Technical Cover containing EMD, Power of Attorney, complete
    Letter of Technical Bid with attachments, qualification documents, and technical details without any financial
    terms.
  evidence_ids:
  - J5d7b86a9eab037bf
  - S09ca218dbb164dfb
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C49f1e4af51e90835
  citations:
  - id: J5d7b86a9eab037bf
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e3cf38f3c94155bb807c3dc1374cc20588ab715e06455bf88cda54b8e92b1665
    heading: Clause 9.0 — Composition of Tender Submittals
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 13
    - 14
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/8
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"9.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"\
      9.0      COMPOSITION OF TENDER SUBMITTALS\\n\\n  9.1       The Tenderer's tender submittals shall comprise\
      \ of (a) Technical Bid (Cover) containing the\\n           documents listed in sub-clause 9.2 of Instructions\
      \ to Tenderers (ITT)) and (b) Financial Bid\\n             (Cover) containing the documents listed in sub-clause\
      \ 9.4 of Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n  9.2      TECHNICAL BID\\n\\n  9.2.1     The Tenderer shall\
      \ upload soft copies (in .pdf format) of the duly completed Technical Bid on\\n             the e-Procurement\
      \ Portal under the “Technical Cover.” The Technical Cover shall comprise\\n             the documents and\
      \ information specified herein.\\n\\n               (a)   Copy of Tender Security (EMD) in accordance with\
      \ Clause 13 of Instructions to\\n                  Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n               (b)   Copy of Power\
      \  of Attorney authorizing the signatory to commit the Tender,  in\\n                 accordance with sub\
      \ clause 14.1 of Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n                (c)   The Letter of Technical Bid (including\
      \ all Attachments) shall be complete without any\\n                      alterations to their content/format.\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"(d)   Documentary evidence/ qualification documents\
      \ required to establish the Tenderer’s\\n                             qualifications under Eligibility and\
      \ qualification criteria.\\n\\n                       (e)    ‘Tenderer’s Brief of Technical Details’ for performing\
      \ the assignment/work as per\\n                        Attachment 14 to Letter of Technical bid.\\n\\n   \
      \                          (f)   Any other documents requested in the Bid document as a part of the Technical\
      \ Bid.\\n\\n          9.2.2     The technical bid must have all pages sequentially numbered, with an index\
      \ sheet indicating\\n                    the corresponding page numbers. Tenderers should note that submitting\
      \ a copy of the\\n                  tender documents downloaded from the e-Procurement portal is not required.\\\
      n\\n         9.3      No information relating to Financial Terms of Service, Conditions, or Prices should\
      \ be\\n                  included in the Technical bid. Any such violation will result in the rejection of\
      \ the Tender.\\n\\n         9.4      FINANCIAL BID\\n\\n          9.4.1     The Tenderer shall upload soft\
      \ copies (.pdf/ .xls) of Financial Proposal duly completed/filled\\n                 on the e-Procurement\
      \ Portal in the ‘Financial Cover’ which shall comprise of following:\\n\\n                         (a) The\
      \ Letter of Price Bid shall be completed without any alterations to their content/\\n\\n                 \
      \         format.\\n\\n                         (b) Price Bid (.xls format) shall be completed as per clause\
      \ 10.1 of ITT.\\n\\n          9.4.2      Bidders shall submit their Price bid only in the format (.xls format)\
      \ provided and no other\\n                    format is acceptable. If the Price bid format is found to be\
      \ modified by the bidder, the bid may\\n                 be rejected.\\n\\n         9.5      The  list of\
      \ documents to be submitted, as described under Clauses 9.2 and 9.4 of the\\n                      Instructions\
      \ to Tenderers (ITT), has been provided solely for the convenience of the Tenderer.\\n                Any\
      \ omission or oversight in this list shall not relieve the Tenderer of the responsibility to\\n          \
      \          thoroughly review all provisions of the Tender Documents and to submit all information and\\n \
      \                documents specifically required or implied therein.\\n\\n             BMRCL shall not take\
      \ cognizance  of, nor be bound by, any additional information or\\n                 documents submitted by\
      \ the Tenderer if, in the opinion of BMRCL, such information is not\\n                    required or is likely\
      \ to materially affect the tendering process.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"9.0      COMPOSITION OF\
      \ TENDER SUBMITTALS\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
    heading_alignment: exact_number_match

- kind: summary
  text: Bidders must ensure that all required tender security instruments and bidding documents are fully uploaded
    and submitted before the official deadline displayed on the e-procurement portal dashboard.
  evidence_ids:
  - J2cf6807848ed47fc
  - Jce685766e7843ace
  - J584b82a80c4bdee5
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C515cefb2f79ff855
  citations:
  - id: J2cf6807848ed47fc
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e3cf38f3c94155bb807c3dc1374cc20588ab715e06455bf88cda54b8e92b1665
    heading: Clause 15.0 — Submission of Tenders
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 19
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/14
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"15.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"\
      15.0      Submission of Tenders\\n\\n  15.1     The tenderer shall submit his tender i.e. Technical Bid and\
      \ Financial Bid respectively on the\\n            e-Procurement Portal mentioned in NIT before the closing\
      \ time on the scheduled last date as\\n              indicated in NIT or as mentioned in subsequent addendum/corrigendum.\
      \ The Technical and\\n              Financial Bids shall be complete in line with clause 9.2 & clause 9.4\
      \ above.\\n\\n  15.2         All documents which are to be uploaded/ submitted as soft copies/ scanned documents\
      \ shall\\n           be typed or written in indelible ink and all the pages shall be signed by the authorized\
      \ signatory\\n               of the Tenderer before scanning and uploading. The employer is not responsible\
      \ for any\\n            misplacement or non-uploading of any required documents to qualify in the bidding\
      \ process.\\n\\n  15.3     The Tenderer shall include with his Tender an index that cross-refers all the Employer’s\\\
      n             tender requirements elaborated in these documents to  all the individual sections within\\n\
      \             Technical Bid and Financial Bid, which the Tenderer intends to be the responses to every one\\\
      n               of those requirements. The Tender submittals i.e. Technical Bid and Financial Bid must be\\\
      n             presented, all pages numbered, and laid out in a logical sequence with main and subheadings\\\
      n               to facilitate evaluation.\\n\\n  15.4     The server time (which is displayed on the bidder’s\
      \ dashboard) will be considered as\\n            the standard time for referencing the deadlines for submission\
      \ of the bids by the\\n             bidders, opening of bids,  etc. The bidders should follow  this time during\
      \ bid\\n            submission.\\n\\n  15.5     The Employer may, at his discretion, extend the date for the\
      \ submission of the Tender by\\n           amending the Tender Documents in which case all rights and obligations\
      \ of the Employer and\\n             the Tenderer previously subject to the original date shall thereafter\
      \ be subject to the new\\n             deadline as extended.\\n\\n  15.6     Upon the successful and timely\
      \ submission of bids (i.e. after Clicking “Freeze Bid Submission”\\n                in the portal), the portal\
      \ will give a successful bid submission message & a bid summary will\\n           be displayed with the bid\
      \ number and the date & time of submission of the bid with all other\\n              relevant details.\\n\\\
      n  15.7     The Tenderer shall ensure that a receipt/acknowledgment is obtained for the submission of\\n \
      \              his tender at the e-Procurement Portal.\\n\\n  15.8     The Tenderer shall note that, ‘No Physical\
      \ Tender will be accepted’.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"15.0      Submission of Tenders\",\n  \"\
      review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
    heading_alignment: exact_number_match
  - id: Jce685766e7843ace
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e3cf38f3c94155bb807c3dc1374cc20588ab715e06455bf88cda54b8e92b1665
    heading: Clause 13.0 — Tender Security (EMD)
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 15
    - 16
    - 17
    - 18
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/12
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"13.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"\
      13.0     TENDER SECURITY (EMD)\\n\\n\\n  13.1      The Tenderer shall submit a Tender Security (Earnest Money\
      \ Deposit – EMD) for the amount\\n               specified in the Notice Inviting Tender (NIT). The Tender\
      \ Security shall be furnished in the\\n             form of an irrevocable and unconditional Bank Guarantee\
      \ (Physical or e-BG; Preferably e-\\n           BG) or an Insurance Surety Bond or Demand Draft or Banker’s\
      \ Cheque or online payment\\n            by NEFT/RTGS, in accordance with the provisions set out below.\\\
      n\\n                       i.     Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG):\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) shall be issued by an Indian Scheduled Bank or a\\n            Scheduled Foreign Bank (as defined\
      \ in Section 2(e) of the Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934),\\n              excluding Co-operative Banks. In\
      \ the case of a Foreign Bank having no branch in India, the\\n         BG (Physical or e-BG) shall be accompanied\
      \ by a counter-guarantee from a Scheduled\\n          Bank in India.\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) shall be payable in India in favour of Bangalore\\n            Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL)\
      \ and shall conform to the prescribed format provided\\n                 in Attachment–12 of the Letter of\
      \ Technical Bid.\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) shall remain valid for a period of\
      \ sixty (60) days\\n           beyond the Tender Validity Period, including any extensions under ITT Sub-Clause\
      \ 12.2\\n            and shall have been issued prior to Tender submission deadline (in case of e-BG,  it\
      \ shall\\n              also be fully accessible on the NeSL portal prior to Tender submission deadline).\\\
      n\\n           The Bank that is issuing the BG shall be on the “Structured Financing Messaging System\\n \
      \           (SFMS)” platform. Separate advice of the BG shall be sent by the issuing Bank to the\\n      \
      \       Employer’s Bank through SFMS to ensure a secure BG. For “SFMS” the following details are\\n      \
      \         to be considered:\\n\\n                 Name: BANGALORE METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED\\n      \
      \         Name of the Bank: BANK OF INDIA\\n                     Branch: CANTONMENT BRANCH, BENGALURU.\\n\
      \                    Account No. 840420110000760\\n                  RTGS/IFSC: BKID0008404\\n           Copy\
      \ of Bank Guarantee along with a copy of MT760COV (for Bank Guarantee message) /\\n          MT767COV (for\
      \ Bank Guarantee amendment message) report sent by the issuing Bank shall\\n            be submitted by the\
      \ Tenderer as a part of their Bidding documents on e-Procurement Portal.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 16,\n      \"text\": \"Banks shall use the following details for issuing e-BG on the NeSL Portal:\\n\\n\
      \                   PAN of BMRCL (Beneficiary): AAACB4881D\\n                            E-mail ID of Beneficiary:\
      \ yogish_nayak@bmrc.co.in\\n                         Tender Reference No.: (Tender number as mentioned in\
      \ NIT)\\n\\n                                   ii.     Insurance Surety Bond:\\n\\n                The Insurance\
      \ Surety Bond shall be issued by an Insurance Company authorised by the\\n                   Insurance Regulatory\
      \ and Development Authority of India (IRDAI) and shall be payable in\\n                      India in favour\
      \ of Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL).\\n\\n                                  It shall conform\
      \ to the prescribed format provided in Attachment–12A of the Letter of\\n                    Technical Bid\
      \ and shall remain valid for a period of sixty (60) days beyond the Tender\\n                     Validity\
      \ Period, including any extensions under ITT Sub-Clause 12.2.\\n\\n                The Insurance Surety Bond\
      \ shall be issued prior to the Tender submission deadline.\\n\\n                                   iii.  \
      \  Demand Draft or Banker’s Cheque:\\n\\n               Demand Draft/ Banker’s Cheque drawn prior to the Tender\
      \ submission deadline in favour of\\n                   Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited payable at\
      \ Bengaluru issued from a Scheduled/\\n                     Nationalised Bank in India.\\n\\n            \
      \              iv.    NEFT/RTGS:\\n\\n               NEFT/RTGS payment shall be made prior to the Tender submission\
      \ deadline, to the BMRCL\\n                   account given below:\\n\\n                      Name: BANGALORE\
      \ METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED\\n                    Name of the Bank: BANK OF INDIA\\n                \
      \           Branch: CANTONMENT BRANCH, BENGALURU.\\n                          Account No. 840420110000760\\\
      n                        RTGS/IFSC: BKID0008404\\n\\n         13.1.1      In Case of Tender Security (EMD)\
      \ in the form of Physical Bank Guarantee or Insurance\\n                    Surety Bond or Demand Draft or\
      \ Banker’s Cheque, the Tenderer shall submit the original\\n                  Tender Security (EMD) to BMRCL\
      \ within the due date & time stipulated in Notice Inviting\\n                  Tender (NIT) (or as amended,\
      \ if applicable).\\n\\n         13.2       In case Joint Venture (JV) participation is permitted in this tender,\
      \ Tender Security (EMD) can\\n                 be furnished by the intended JV or any member of the JV.\\\
      n         13.3     As part of the tender submission, the Tenderer shall upload a copy of the Tender Security\\\
      n                (EMD) on e-Procurement Portal.\\n\\n                                           i.    For\
      \ the purpose of tender evaluation and all subsequent contractual proceedings, the\\n\\n                 \
      \            authentic and legally binding version of the Tender Security shall be:\\n\\n                \
      \               (a) The original Physical Bank Guarantee received by BMRCL; or\\n\\n                     \
      \          (b) The version electronically received by BMRCL through direct intimation from the\\n        \
      \               NeSL portal (in the case of an e-BG); or\\n\\n                                (c) The original\
      \ physical Insurance Surety Bond received by BMRCL; or\\n\\n                               (d) The original\
      \ Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque received by BMRCL (subject to\\n                                   realisation\
      \ of amount in BMRCL account); or\\n\\n                               (e) The amount received  in the prescribed\
      \ BMRCL account vide NEFT/RTGS\\n                          payment made by the tenderer before the deadline\
      \ for submission of tender.\\n\\n                                            ii.    In the event of any discrepancy\
      \ between the uploaded copy and the authoritative\\n\\n                              version, the following\
      \ shall prevail:\\n\\n                               (a) For a Physical Bank Guarantee, the original physical\
      \ document received by\\n                       BMRCL.\\n\\n                               (b) For an e-BG,\
      \ the version confirmed by the NeSL portal.\\n\\n                                (c) For an Insurance Surety\
      \ Bond, the original physical document received by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 17,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"BMRCL.\\n\\n                       (d) For a Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque, the original physical document\
      \ received\\n                     by BMRCL.\\n\\n                       (e) For NEFT/RTGS payment made by\
      \ the tenderer, amount received in prescribed\\n                BMRCL account before the deadline for submission\
      \ of tender.\\n\\n   13.4     The Employer shall summarily reject the Tender in any of the following cases:\\\
      n\\n                           (i)   If the required Tender Security (EMD) is not effectively provided:\\\
      n\\n                       (a) For a Physical Bank Guarantee:  If the  original physical document as per\\\
      n                       requirement is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n \
      \                       Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n               \
      \        (b) For an e-Bank Guarantee (e-BG):  If  it  is not accessible to BMRCL as per\\n               \
      \        requirement on the NeSL portal within the deadline for submission of tender\\n                  \
      \        stipulated in Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n                \
      \        (c) For an Insurance Surety Bond:  If the  original physical document as per\\n                 \
      \      requirement is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n                  \
      \      Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n                       (d) For a\
      \ Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque: If the original physical document as per\\n                       requirement\
      \ is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n                        Notice Inviting\
      \ Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable) and if the submitted\\n                  Demand Draft / Banker’s\
      \ Cheque is not realized in BMRCL account.\\n\\n                       (e) For a NEFT/RTGS: Required amount\
      \ is not received in the prescribed BMRCL\\n                       account before the deadline for submission\
      \ of tender.\\n\\n                             (ii)  If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) or Insurance\
      \ Surety Bond or Demand Draft\\n\\n                      or Banker’s Cheque is issued after the tender submission\
      \ deadline.\\n\\n                              (iii)  If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) is issued by\
      \ any bank other than an Indian\\n\\n                   Scheduled Bank or a Scheduled Foreign Bank as defined\
      \ under Section 2(e) of the\\n\\n                  Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934, read with the Second Schedule\
      \ thereto.\\n\\n                       (iv) If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) is issued by a Co-operative\
      \ Bank.\\n\\n                     (v)  If the Insurance Surety Bond is issued by an insurance company not\
      \ authorized by\\n\\n                     the Insurance Regulatory and Development Authority of India (IRDAI).\\\
      n\\n                       (vi) If the furnished amount of the Tender Security (EMD) is less than the amount\\\
      n\\n                      specified in the Notice Inviting Tender (NIT).\\n\\n                         (vii)\
      \ If the validity period of the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) or Insurance Surety\\n\\n              \
      \   Bond is shorter than the Tender validity period required under Clause 12.0 of the\\n\\n              \
      \         Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n  13.4.1        If the validity of the Tender Security (Bank\
      \ Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) / Insurance Surety\\n            Bond) submitted by the Tenderer meets the\
      \ prescribed Tender Validity Period but does not\\n            conform to the required Tender Security Validity\
      \ Period, the Employer shall notify the\\n            Tenderer in writing through the eProcurement Portal\
      \ or by e-mail/post, directing submission\\n               of a compliant Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG)\
      \  / Insurance Surety Bond within fifteen (15)\\n             working days from the date of such notification.\\\
      n\\n              Failure of the Tenderer to furnish a compliant Tender Security within the stipulated period\
      \ shall\\n               result in the Tender being rejected and declared non-responsive.\\n\\n  13.4.2  \
      \  Where the Employer, in its sole discretion, extends the deadline for submission of Bids in\\n         \
      \   accordance  with ITT Clause  15.5,  after commencement  of  Bid submission on  the\\n            eProcurement\
      \ Portal, any Bid already submitted shall remain valid, subject to compliance\\n              with the revised\
      \ Tender Security Validity Period. In the event that the Tender Security (Bank\\n            Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) / Insurance Surety Bond) submitted with such Bid does not\\n            conform to the revised\
      \ validity requirements,  if necessary, the Employer shall notify the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 18,\n      \"text\": \"concerned Tenderer in writing, through the eProcurement Portal or by e-mail/post,\
      \ and\\n                     require submission of a compliant Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) / Insurance\
      \ Surety\\n                Bond within fifteen (15) working days from the date of issuance of such notice.\\\
      n\\n                     Failure of the Tenderer to furnish a compliant Tender Security within the stipulated\
      \ period\\n                       shall result in the tender being rejected and declared non-responsive.\\\
      n\\n         13.5      Tender Securities (EMD) of the non-responsive or unsuccessful bidders will be returned\
      \ in\\n                  accordance with sub-clause 22.1.7 or 25.3 of Instruction to Tenderers (ITT) respectively\
      \ as\\n                    the case may be.\\n\\n         13.6     The Tender Security (EMD) of the Successful\
      \ Tenderer shall be returned upon the Tenderer\\n                    executing the Contract Agreement after\
      \ furnishing the required Performance guarantee as\\n                   per the Contract.\\n\\n         13.7\
      \     The Tender Security (EMD) shall be forfeited;\\n\\n                       (a)       if a Tenderer withdraws\
      \ his tender during the period of tender validity;\\n\\n                       (b)       if the tenderer does\
      \ not accept the correction of his tendered price in terms of Clause\\n                          22.2.5 of\
      \ Instructions to Tenderers (ITT);\\n\\n                        (c)       if a tenderer misrepresents or omits\
      \ the facts to influence the procurement process.\\n\\n                       (d)    in the case of a successful\
      \ tenderer if he fails to;\\n\\n                                         (i)    Furnish the necessary Performance\
      \ guarantee.\\n\\n                                            (ii)    Enter into Contract within the time\
      \ limit specified.\\n\\n                       (e)    In case Joint Venture (JV) participation is permitted\
      \ in this tender and if any, JV member/s\\n                          withdraws from the JV after submission\
      \ of the tender.\\n\\n         13.8     No interest will be payable by the Employer on the Tender Security\
      \ amount.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"13.0     TENDER SECURITY (EMD)\",\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\"\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"16.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"\
      16.0     FAILURE TO SUBMIT TENDERS IN TIME:\\n\\n            Submission of Tenders shall be closed on the\
      \ eProcurement Portal at the last date & time of\\n            submission prescribed in NIT or as mentioned\
      \ in subsequent addendum/corrigendum after\\n            which no tender shall be accepted by the eProcurement\
      \ portal.\\n\\n         BMRCL will not be responsible for any delay, internet connection failure, or any error\\\
      n              in uploading the tender. The tenderers are advised to upload their submissions well\\n    \
      \        before the due date and time of tender submission to avoid any problems and last-\\n           minute\
      \ rush.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"16.0     FAILURE TO SUBMIT TENDERS IN TIME:\",\n  \"review_status\"\
      : \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: If an initial tender security is valid for the tender validity period but does not cover the required security
    validity period, BMRCL will notify the bidder in writing to furnish a compliant instrument within fifteen working
    days.
  evidence_ids:

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"13.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"\
      13.0     TENDER SECURITY (EMD)\\n\\n\\n  13.1      The Tenderer shall submit a Tender Security (Earnest Money\
      \ Deposit – EMD) for the amount\\n               specified in the Notice Inviting Tender (NIT). The Tender\
      \ Security shall be furnished in the\\n             form of an irrevocable and unconditional Bank Guarantee\
      \ (Physical or e-BG; Preferably e-\\n           BG) or an Insurance Surety Bond or Demand Draft or Banker’s\
      \ Cheque or online payment\\n            by NEFT/RTGS, in accordance with the provisions set out below.\\\
      n\\n                       i.     Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG):\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) shall be issued by an Indian Scheduled Bank or a\\n            Scheduled Foreign Bank (as defined\
      \ in Section 2(e) of the Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934),\\n              excluding Co-operative Banks. In\
      \ the case of a Foreign Bank having no branch in India, the\\n         BG (Physical or e-BG) shall be accompanied\
      \ by a counter-guarantee from a Scheduled\\n          Bank in India.\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) shall be payable in India in favour of Bangalore\\n            Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL)\
      \ and shall conform to the prescribed format provided\\n                 in Attachment–12 of the Letter of\
      \ Technical Bid.\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) shall remain valid for a period of\
      \ sixty (60) days\\n           beyond the Tender Validity Period, including any extensions under ITT Sub-Clause\
      \ 12.2\\n            and shall have been issued prior to Tender submission deadline (in case of e-BG,  it\
      \ shall\\n              also be fully accessible on the NeSL portal prior to Tender submission deadline).\\\
      n\\n           The Bank that is issuing the BG shall be on the “Structured Financing Messaging System\\n \
      \           (SFMS)” platform. Separate advice of the BG shall be sent by the issuing Bank to the\\n      \
      \       Employer’s Bank through SFMS to ensure a secure BG. For “SFMS” the following details are\\n      \
      \         to be considered:\\n\\n                 Name: BANGALORE METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED\\n      \
      \         Name of the Bank: BANK OF INDIA\\n                     Branch: CANTONMENT BRANCH, BENGALURU.\\n\
      \                    Account No. 840420110000760\\n                  RTGS/IFSC: BKID0008404\\n           Copy\
      \ of Bank Guarantee along with a copy of MT760COV (for Bank Guarantee message) /\\n          MT767COV (for\
      \ Bank Guarantee amendment message) report sent by the issuing Bank shall\\n            be submitted by the\
      \ Tenderer as a part of their Bidding documents on e-Procurement Portal.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 16,\n      \"text\": \"Banks shall use the following details for issuing e-BG on the NeSL Portal:\\n\\n\
      \                   PAN of BMRCL (Beneficiary): AAACB4881D\\n                            E-mail ID of Beneficiary:\
      \ yogish_nayak@bmrc.co.in\\n                         Tender Reference No.: (Tender number as mentioned in\
      \ NIT)\\n\\n                                   ii.     Insurance Surety Bond:\\n\\n                The Insurance\
      \ Surety Bond shall be issued by an Insurance Company authorised by the\\n                   Insurance Regulatory\
      \ and Development Authority of India (IRDAI) and shall be payable in\\n                      India in favour\
      \ of Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL).\\n\\n                                  It shall conform\
      \ to the prescribed format provided in Attachment–12A of the Letter of\\n                    Technical Bid\
      \ and shall remain valid for a period of sixty (60) days beyond the Tender\\n                     Validity\
      \ Period, including any extensions under ITT Sub-Clause 12.2.\\n\\n                The Insurance Surety Bond\
      \ shall be issued prior to the Tender submission deadline.\\n\\n                                   iii.  \
      \  Demand Draft or Banker’s Cheque:\\n\\n               Demand Draft/ Banker’s Cheque drawn prior to the Tender\
      \ submission deadline in favour of\\n                   Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited payable at\
      \ Bengaluru issued from a Scheduled/\\n                     Nationalised Bank in India.\\n\\n            \
      \              iv.    NEFT/RTGS:\\n\\n               NEFT/RTGS payment shall be made prior to the Tender submission\
      \ deadline, to the BMRCL\\n                   account given below:\\n\\n                      Name: BANGALORE\
      \ METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED\\n                    Name of the Bank: BANK OF INDIA\\n                \
      \           Branch: CANTONMENT BRANCH, BENGALURU.\\n                          Account No. 840420110000760\\\
      n                        RTGS/IFSC: BKID0008404\\n\\n         13.1.1      In Case of Tender Security (EMD)\
      \ in the form of Physical Bank Guarantee or Insurance\\n                    Surety Bond or Demand Draft or\
      \ Banker’s Cheque, the Tenderer shall submit the original\\n                  Tender Security (EMD) to BMRCL\
      \ within the due date & time stipulated in Notice Inviting\\n                  Tender (NIT) (or as amended,\
      \ if applicable).\\n\\n         13.2       In case Joint Venture (JV) participation is permitted in this tender,\
      \ Tender Security (EMD) can\\n                 be furnished by the intended JV or any member of the JV.\\\
      n         13.3     As part of the tender submission, the Tenderer shall upload a copy of the Tender Security\\\
      n                (EMD) on e-Procurement Portal.\\n\\n                                           i.    For\
      \ the purpose of tender evaluation and all subsequent contractual proceedings, the\\n\\n                 \
      \            authentic and legally binding version of the Tender Security shall be:\\n\\n                \
      \               (a) The original Physical Bank Guarantee received by BMRCL; or\\n\\n                     \
      \          (b) The version electronically received by BMRCL through direct intimation from the\\n        \
      \               NeSL portal (in the case of an e-BG); or\\n\\n                                (c) The original\
      \ physical Insurance Surety Bond received by BMRCL; or\\n\\n                               (d) The original\
      \ Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque received by BMRCL (subject to\\n                                   realisation\
      \ of amount in BMRCL account); or\\n\\n                               (e) The amount received  in the prescribed\
      \ BMRCL account vide NEFT/RTGS\\n                          payment made by the tenderer before the deadline\
      \ for submission of tender.\\n\\n                                            ii.    In the event of any discrepancy\
      \ between the uploaded copy and the authoritative\\n\\n                              version, the following\
      \ shall prevail:\\n\\n                               (a) For a Physical Bank Guarantee, the original physical\
      \ document received by\\n                       BMRCL.\\n\\n                               (b) For an e-BG,\
      \ the version confirmed by the NeSL portal.\\n\\n                                (c) For an Insurance Surety\
      \ Bond, the original physical document received by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 17,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"BMRCL.\\n\\n                       (d) For a Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque, the original physical document\
      \ received\\n                     by BMRCL.\\n\\n                       (e) For NEFT/RTGS payment made by\
      \ the tenderer, amount received in prescribed\\n                BMRCL account before the deadline for submission\
      \ of tender.\\n\\n   13.4     The Employer shall summarily reject the Tender in any of the following cases:\\\
      n\\n                           (i)   If the required Tender Security (EMD) is not effectively provided:\\\
      n\\n                       (a) For a Physical Bank Guarantee:  If the  original physical document as per\\\
      n                       requirement is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n \
      \                       Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n               \
      \        (b) For an e-Bank Guarantee (e-BG):  If  it  is not accessible to BMRCL as per\\n               \
      \        requirement on the NeSL portal within the deadline for submission of tender\\n                  \
      \        stipulated in Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n                \
      \        (c) For an Insurance Surety Bond:  If the  original physical document as per\\n                 \
      \      requirement is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n                  \
      \      Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n                       (d) For a\
      \ Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque: If the original physical document as per\\n                       requirement\
      \ is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n                        Notice Inviting\
      \ Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable) and if the submitted\\n                  Demand Draft / Banker’s\
      \ Cheque is not realized in BMRCL account.\\n\\n                       (e) For a NEFT/RTGS: Required amount\
      \ is not received in the prescribed BMRCL\\n                       account before the deadline for submission\
      \ of tender.\\n\\n                             (ii)  If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) or Insurance\
      \ Surety Bond or Demand Draft\\n\\n                      or Banker’s Cheque is issued after the tender submission\
      \ deadline.\\n\\n                              (iii)  If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) is issued by\
      \ any bank other than an Indian\\n\\n                   Scheduled Bank or a Scheduled Foreign Bank as defined\
      \ under Section 2(e) of the\\n\\n                  Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934, read with the Second Schedule\
      \ thereto.\\n\\n                       (iv) If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) is issued by a Co-operative\
      \ Bank.\\n\\n                     (v)  If the Insurance Surety Bond is issued by an insurance company not\
      \ authorized by\\n\\n                     the Insurance Regulatory and Development Authority of India (IRDAI).\\\
      n\\n                       (vi) If the furnished amount of the Tender Security (EMD) is less than the amount\\\
      n\\n                      specified in the Notice Inviting Tender (NIT).\\n\\n                         (vii)\
      \ If the validity period of the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) or Insurance Surety\\n\\n              \
      \   Bond is shorter than the Tender validity period required under Clause 12.0 of the\\n\\n              \
      \         Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n  13.4.1        If the validity of the Tender Security (Bank\
      \ Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) / Insurance Surety\\n            Bond) submitted by the Tenderer meets the\
      \ prescribed Tender Validity Period but does not\\n            conform to the required Tender Security Validity\
      \ Period, the Employer shall notify the\\n            Tenderer in writing through the eProcurement Portal\
      \ or by e-mail/post, directing submission\\n               of a compliant Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG)\
      \  / Insurance Surety Bond within fifteen (15)\\n             working days from the date of such notification.\\\
      n\\n              Failure of the Tenderer to furnish a compliant Tender Security within the stipulated period\
      \ shall\\n               result in the Tender being rejected and declared non-responsive.\\n\\n  13.4.2  \
      \  Where the Employer, in its sole discretion, extends the deadline for submission of Bids in\\n         \
      \   accordance  with ITT Clause  15.5,  after commencement  of  Bid submission on  the\\n            eProcurement\
      \ Portal, any Bid already submitted shall remain valid, subject to compliance\\n              with the revised\
      \ Tender Security Validity Period. In the event that the Tender Security (Bank\\n            Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) / Insurance Surety Bond) submitted with such Bid does not\\n            conform to the revised\
      \ validity requirements,  if necessary, the Employer shall notify the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 18,\n      \"text\": \"concerned Tenderer in writing, through the eProcurement Portal or by e-mail/post,\
      \ and\\n                     require submission of a compliant Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) / Insurance\
      \ Surety\\n                Bond within fifteen (15) working days from the date of issuance of such notice.\\\
      n\\n                     Failure of the Tenderer to furnish a compliant Tender Security within the stipulated\
      \ period\\n                       shall result in the tender being rejected and declared non-responsive.\\\
      n\\n         13.5      Tender Securities (EMD) of the non-responsive or unsuccessful bidders will be returned\
      \ in\\n                  accordance with sub-clause 22.1.7 or 25.3 of Instruction to Tenderers (ITT) respectively\
      \ as\\n                    the case may be.\\n\\n         13.6     The Tender Security (EMD) of the Successful\
      \ Tenderer shall be returned upon the Tenderer\\n                    executing the Contract Agreement after\
      \ furnishing the required Performance guarantee as\\n                   per the Contract.\\n\\n         13.7\
      \     The Tender Security (EMD) shall be forfeited;\\n\\n                       (a)       if a Tenderer withdraws\
      \ his tender during the period of tender validity;\\n\\n                       (b)       if the tenderer does\
      \ not accept the correction of his tendered price in terms of Clause\\n                          22.2.5 of\
      \ Instructions to Tenderers (ITT);\\n\\n                        (c)       if a tenderer misrepresents or omits\
      \ the facts to influence the procurement process.\\n\\n                       (d)    in the case of a successful\
      \ tenderer if he fails to;\\n\\n                                         (i)    Furnish the necessary Performance\
      \ guarantee.\\n\\n                                            (ii)    Enter into Contract within the time\
      \ limit specified.\\n\\n                       (e)    In case Joint Venture (JV) participation is permitted\
      \ in this tender and if any, JV member/s\\n                          withdraws from the JV after submission\
      \ of the tender.\\n\\n         13.8     No interest will be payable by the Employer on the Tender Security\
      \ amount.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"13.0     TENDER SECURITY (EMD)\",\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\"\n}"

- kind: summary
  text: Aggrieved bidders may appeal under Section 16 of the KTPP Act to the Appellate Authority, which is the Managing
    Director of BMRCL, before initiating legal proceedings in the courts at Bangalore.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jcb7b6d174a0a2144
  - Scd85c70e67fd35e1

      30.0     APPEAL\\n\\n  30.1      Under section 16 of the KTPP Act, any aggrieved Bidder may appeal, within\

      : \"30.0     APPEAL\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"

      26.0     SIGNING OF CONTRACT AGREEMENT\\n\\n         26.1     The Employer shall prepare the Contract Agreement\
      \ in the format as prescribed in Contract\\n                Forms (Form-1), with such modifications as may\
      \ be necessary for duly incorporating all the\\n                   terms of the agreement between the two\
      \ parties. The successful Tenderer will be required to\\n                   execute the Contract Agreement\
      \ within the period stated in Contract Data of General\\n                    Conditions of Contract (GCC).\
      \ One copy of the Contract Agreement duly signed by the\\n                  Employer and the Tenderer through\
      \ their authorized signatories will be supplied by the\\n                  Employer to the Tenderer.\"\n \
      \   }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"26.0     SIGNING OF CONTRACT AGREEMENT\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\

- kind: summary
  text: Tenderers must satisfy strict eligibility and qualification criteria, including prohibitions on joint ventures,
    compliance with class-I local supplier local content rules, land-border restrictions, and specific exclusions.
  evidence_ids:
  - J6d3ed99d534c4170
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C8fb96acc16cdee4c
  citations:
  - id: J6d3ed99d534c4170
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC.md
    okf_sha256: c658fe17e72740fef6f9e4825097f339eb9cf93901ef98a23761ba54b3b4b04d
    heading: Clause 2.0 — Eligible Tenderers
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 27
    - 28
    - 29
    - 30
    - 31
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_clauses.json
    json_sha256: d8207622663bc2dd0cf2a7c5e3ed660f5c3881838e376d1bbb26fdae47ed8abf
    json_pointer: /clauses/1
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"2.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"\
      2.0     ELIGIBLE TENDERERS\\n\\n\\n       2.1.   Tenderer may be a Proprietorship Firm/ Partnership Firm/Private\
      \ Limited Company/ Public\\n              Limited Company. Tenderer shall apply as a Single Entity. Joint\
      \ Venture/Consortium is\\n            not permitted in this tender.\\n\\n       2.2.   Only those Tenderers\
      \ who satisfy the condition of ‘Class-I Local Supplier’ as per Clause\\n              2.3 of EQC shall be\
      \ eligible to participate in this Tender.\\n\\n       2.3.    ‘Class-I Local Supplier’ means a supplier or\
      \ service provider whose goods, services or works\\n              offered for procurement, meets the minimum\
      \ Local content of 90%.\\n\\n       2.4.    ‘Local content’ means the amount of value added in India which\
      \ shall, be the total value of\\n              the item procured (excluding net domestic indirect taxes) minus\
      \ the value of imported content\\n                 in the item (including all customs duties) as proportion\
      \ of the total value, in percent.\\n\\n       2.5.   The minimum Local Content for this service shall be certified\
      \ as below;\\n\\n                (a) The ‘Class-I Local Supplier’ at the time of Bid submission shall provide\
      \ a Declaration\\n                    that the item offered meets the minimum ‘Local Content’ and shall give\
      \ details of the\\n                    location(s) at which the local value addition is made. (Attachment-1\
      \ to Letter of Technical\\n                    Bid).\\n\\n                (b) DELETED\\n\\n       2.6.   Bidder\
      \ claiming as ‘Class-I Local Supplier is exempted from meeting the stipulated\\n             ‘Local content’\
      \ in case, if the product is being manufactured in India under a license from a\\n               foreign manufacturer,\
      \ who holds intellectual property rights and where there is a technology\\n               collaboration agreement/transfer\
      \ of technology agreement for indigenous manufacture of a\\n             product developed abroad with clear\
      \ phasing of increase in local content, subject to\\n             submission of necessary documents.\\n\\\
      n       2.7.   Bidder  shall note  that  in case  of ‘Imported Products’, the related services such as\\n\
      \               transportation, insurance,  installation, commissioning, training and after sales services\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 28,\n      \"text\": \"support like AMC/CMC etc., shall not be claimed\
      \ as Local value addition. Such services will\\n              not be considered as ‘Local Content’.\\n\\n\
      \       2.8.   A committee  with  Internal and  external experts  for Independent  verification  of  self-\\\
      n              Declarations by the Bidder may be constituted by BMRCL on random basis and in case of\\n  \
      \            complaints.\\n\\n       2.9.    In case of false declarations by a Bidder claiming as Class-I\
      \ Local Supplier, the following\\n              actions will be taken;\\n\\n                (a) The proposal\
      \ submitted by the Bidder will be rejected.\\n\\n                (b) The Bidder will be debarred for up to\
      \ Two years.\\n\\n       2.10.  Any Bidder from a country which shares a land border with India will be eligible\
      \ to bid in this\\n             Bid only  if the Bidder  is registered with the Competent Authority. Further,\
      \ any bidder\\n               (including from India) having specified Transfer of Technology (ToT) arrangement\
      \ with an\\n                entity from a country which share a land border with India, shall also require\
      \ to be registered\\n              with the same competent authority constituted by Department for Promotion\
      \ of Industry and\\n               Internal Trade (DPIIT), Government of India or the Competent Authority\
      \ constituted by\\n            Government of Karnataka.\\n\\n                          i.  \\\"Bidder\\\"\
      \ (including the term 'Bidder', 'consultant' or 'service provider' in certain contexts)\\n             means\
      \ any person or firm or company, including any member of a consortium or joint\\n                venture (that\
      \ is an association of several persons, or firms or companies), every artificial\\n                    juridical\
      \ person not falling in any of the descriptions of Bidders stated hereinbefore,\\n                  including\
      \ any agency branch or office controlled by such person, participating in a\\n               procurement process.\\\
      n\\n                          ii.  \\\"Bidder from a country which shares a land border with India” means:\\\
      n\\n                    (a) An entity incorporated, established or registered in such a country; or\\n\\n\
      \                    (b) A subsidiary of an entity incorporated, established or registered in such a country;\
      \ or\\n\\n                     (c) An  entity  substantially  controlled through  entities  incorporated,\
      \  established  or\\n                      registered in such a country; or\\n\\n                    (d) An\
      \ entity whose beneficial owner is situated in such a country; or\\n\\n                    (e) An Indian (or\
      \ other) agent of such an entity; or\\n\\n                         (f) A natural person who is a citizen of\
      \ such a country; or\\n\\n                    (g) A consortium or Joint venture where any member of the consortium\
      \ or joint venture\\n                           falls under any of the above.\\n\\n                      \
      \    iii. The beneficial owner for the purpose of (ii) above will be as under:\\n\\n                    (a)\
      \ In case of a company or Limited Liability Partnership, the beneficial owner is the\\n                  \
      \    natural person(s), who, whether acting alone or together, or through one or more\\n                 \
      \         juridical person, has a controlling ownership interest or who exercises control through\\n     \
      \                other means.\\n\\n                         \\\"Controlling ownership interest\\\" means\
      \ ownership of or entitlement to more than\\n                           twenty-five per cent of shares or\
      \ capital or profits of the company;\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 29,\n      \"text\": \"   \\\"\
      Control” shall include the right to appoint majority of the directors or to control\\n                   \
      \      the management or policy decisions including by virtue of their shareholding or\\n                \
      \    management rights or shareholders  agreements  or voting agreements;\\n                    (b) In case\
      \ of a partnership firm, the beneficial owner is the natural person(s) who,\\n                   whether acting\
      \ alone or together, or through one or more juridical person, has\\n                   ownership of entitlement\
      \ to more than fifteen percent of capital or profits of the\\n                      partnership;\\n\\n   \
      \                  (c) In case of an unincorporated association or body of individuals, the beneficial owner\\\
      n                           is the natural person(s), who, whether acting alone or together, or through one\
      \ or\\n                 more juridical person, has ownership of or entitlement to more than fifteen percent\
      \ of\\n                     the property or capital or profits of such association or body of individuals;\\\
      n\\n                    (d) Where no natural person is identified under (a) or (b) or (c) above, the beneficial\\\
      n                  owner is the relevant natural person who holds the position of senior managing\\n     \
      \                       official;\\n\\n                    (e) In case of a trust, the identification  of\
      \ beneficial  owner(s) shall include identification\\n                       of the author of the trust, the\
      \ trustee, the beneficiaries with fifteen percent or more\\n                        interest in the trust\
      \ and any other natural person exercising ultimate effective control\\n                    over the trust\
      \ through a chain of control or ownership.\\n\\n                         (f) An Agent is a person employed\
      \ to do any act for another, or to represent another in\\n                     dealings with third person.\\\
      n\\n                                      i. Successful Bidder shall not be allowed to sub-contract works\
      \ to any contractor from\\n                  a country which shares a land border with India unless such contractor\
      \ is registered\\n                       with the Competent Authority. The definition of “Contractor from\
      \ a country which\\n                     shares a land border with India” shall be as in para (ii) above.\\\
      n                                       ii. The registration shall be valid at the time of submission of bids\
      \ and at the time of\\n                    acceptance of bid.\\n                                       iii.\
      \ If the bidder was validly registered at the time of acceptance / placement of order,\\n                \
      \         registration shall not be relevant consideration during contract execution.\\n       2.11.  Bidder\
      \  is required  to submit undertaking  for compliance  of the above Clause 2.10\\n             (Attachment-1\
      \ to Letter of Technical Bid).\\n\\n       2.12.  The above Clause 2.10 will not apply to Bidders from those\
      \ countries (even if sharing a land\\n             border with India) to which the Government of India has\
      \ extended lines of credit or in which\\n              the Government of India is engaged in development projects.\
      \ Updated lists of countries to\\n             which lines of credit have been extended or in which development\
      \ projects are undertaken\\n             are given in the website of the Ministry of External Affairs.\\n\\\
      n       2.13.  Tenderers shall not have a conflict of interest that affects the Bidding Process. Any Tenderer\\\
      n             found to have a Conflict of Interest shall be disqualified. A Tenderer will be deemed to have\\\
      n               Conflict of interest with one or more parties in this Tendering process, if:\\n\\n       \
      \         (a) A tenderer submits more than one bid either individually or as a member of a JV for a\\n   \
      \             work will result in disqualification of all its bids for that work.\\n\\n                (b)\
      \ The Tenderer has been engaged by the Employer to provide consulting services for the\\n                \
      \  preparation related to procurement for or implementation of this work.\\n\\n                 (c) Any association/affiliation\
      \ (inclusive of parent firms) of the Bidder has been engaged by\\n                  the Employer to provide\
      \ consulting services for the preparation related to procurement\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 30,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"for or implementation of this work.\\n\\n                (d) The Tenderer lends, or temporarily\
      \ seconds its personnel to Firms or organizations\\n                which are engaged in Consulting services\
      \ for the preparation related to procurement for\\n                   or implementation of this work.\\n\\\
      n       2.14.  DELETED\\n\\n       2.15.  As on date of submission of Bid, the Bidder (each member, in case\
      \ of JV) should not be\\n             excluded as per the GROUNDS FOR EXCLUSION. The Bidder should submit\
      \ an\\n              undertaking to this effect.\\n\\n                      (i) GROUNDS FOR EXCLUSION will\
      \ constitute the following, if the Bidders (in case of JV\\n                 applicable to all member(s));\\\
      n\\n                  (a) are bankrupt, being wound up or ceasing their activities, are having their activities\\\
      n                   administered by the courts, have entered into receivership, or are in any analogous\\\
      n                      situation arising from any similar procedure; or\\n\\n                         is under\
      \ insolvency proceedings at the time of bid submission and until the contract is\\n                awarded\
      \ or adjudged insolvent by the National Company Law Tribunal (NCLT), with\\n                 any resolution\
      \ proceedings pending before the NCLT;\\n\\n                  (b) have been convicted within the past five\
      \ years by a court decision, which has the force\\n                      of residential jurisdiction in the\
      \ country where the project is implemented, of fraud or\\n                    corruption or any other offence\
      \ committed during the procurement or performance of a\\n                     contract, unless they provide\
      \ supporting information together with their Statement of\\n                       Integrity which shows that\
      \ this conviction is not relevant in the context of this project;\\n\\n                   (c) have committed\
      \ serious professional misconduct within the past five years during the\\n                 Procurement or\
      \ performance of a contract, as evidenced by any means by the\\n                  Employer;\\n\\n        \
      \          (d) have not fulfilled their obligations regarding the payment of social security contributions\\\
      n                    or taxes in accordance with the legal provisions of the country where they are\\n   \
      \                 established or the Employer ‘s country;\\n\\n                  (e) have indulged in ‘Corrupt\
      \ Practice’; “corrupt practice” means the offering, giving,\\n                    receiving or soliciting\
      \ of anything of value to influence the action of a public official in\\n                   the procurement\
      \ process or in contract execution;\\n\\n                       (f) have indulged in ‘Fraudulent Practice’;\
      \ “fraudulent practice” means a misrepresentation\\n                      of facts in order to influence an\
      \ evaluation process or the execution of a contract, and\\n                    includes misleading or false\
      \ representation in forms or statements or attachment\\n                   submitted and collusive practices\
      \ among Bidders (prior to or after bid submission)\\n                  designed to establish bid prices at\
      \ artificial, non-competitive levels and to deprive the\\n                 Employer of the benefits of free\
      \ and open competitions;\\n\\n                  (g) have been considered ineligible due to execution of Bid–Securing\
      \ Declaration by any\\n                    Central Govt./State Govt./PSUs;\\n\\n                  (h) have\
      \ been Black  listed/debarred /banned business by any Central Govt./State\\n                 Govt./PSUs or\
      \ Local Authority or World Bank or ADB or JICA or any other International\\n                   Financing Institution,\
      \ or any entity controlled by them;\\n\\n                         (i) have left/abandoned the awarded works\
      \ on his own in the last 05 years;\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 31,\n      \"text\": \"(j) “have\
      \ been rescinded / terminated by any Central Govt. or State Govt. or PSUs or\\n                Government\
      \ entity after award, during last 5 years”;\\n\\n                   (k) have been either penalized with liquidated\
      \ damages of 5% (or more) of the contract\\n                   value due to delay or imposed with penalty\
      \ of 5% (or more) of the contract value due\\n                     to any other reason in any Contract awarded\
      \ by BMRCL/ any other Metro Organization\\n                (100% owned by Govt.), during last three years\
      \ (from the day of bid submission);\\n\\n                       (ii) Bidders may also note that in case the\
      \ Bidder is excluded as per Ground for Exclusion\\n               mentioned above, after the due date of submission\
      \ of Bid but before award of the contract\\n               by BMRCL, they shall inform the same to BMRCL in\
      \ writing within 5 working days, failing\\n               which it will be considered that the Bidder has\
      \ wilfully concealed the information and the\\n                Bidder shall be solely responsible for all\
      \ implications that may arise in accordance with\\n                 the conditions of this Bid.\\n\\n    \
      \                    (iii) Any such exclusion will result in disqualification of the Bidder and the financial\
      \ proposal\\n                   of such Bidder shall not be opened in case the technical evaluation is still\
      \ in progress. In\\n               case the Financial Proposals have already been opened, and the work not\
      \ yet awarded,\\n                 the Financial Proposal will not be further considered for evaluation.\"\n\
      \    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Eligible Tenderers\"\n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"2.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"\
      2.0     ELIGIBLE TENDERERS\\n\\n\\n       2.1.   Tenderer may be a Proprietorship Firm/ Partnership Firm/Private\
      \ Limited Company/ Public\\n              Limited Company. Tenderer shall apply as a Single Entity. Joint\
