      \ who satisfy the condition of ‘Class-I Local Supplier’ as per Clause\\n              2.3 of EQC shall be\
      \ eligible to participate in this Tender.\\n\\n       2.3.    ‘Class-I Local Supplier’ means a supplier or\
      \ service provider whose goods, services or works\\n              offered for procurement, meets the minimum\
      \ Local content of 90%.\\n\\n       2.4.    ‘Local content’ means the amount of value added in India which\
      \ shall, be the total value of\\n              the item procured (excluding net domestic indirect taxes) minus\
      \ the value of imported content\\n                 in the item (including all customs duties) as proportion\
      \ of the total value, in percent.\\n\\n       2.5.   The minimum Local Content for this service shall be certified\
      \ as below;\\n\\n                (a) The ‘Class-I Local Supplier’ at the time of Bid submission shall provide\
      \ a Declaration\\n                    that the item offered meets the minimum ‘Local Content’ and shall give\
      \ details of the\\n                    location(s) at which the local value addition is made. (Attachment-1\
      \ to Letter of Technical\\n                    Bid).\\n\\n                (b) DELETED\\n\\n       2.6.   Bidder\
      \ claiming as ‘Class-I Local Supplier is exempted from meeting the stipulated\\n             ‘Local content’\
      \ in case, if the product is being manufactured in India under a license from a\\n               foreign manufacturer,\
      \ who holds intellectual property rights and where there is a technology\\n               collaboration agreement/transfer\
      \ of technology agreement for indigenous manufacture of a\\n             product developed abroad with clear\
      \ phasing of increase in local content, subject to\\n             submission of necessary documents.\\n\\\
      n       2.7.   Bidder  shall note  that  in case  of ‘Imported Products’, the related services such as\\n\
      \               transportation, insurance,  installation, commissioning, training and after sales services\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 28,\n      \"text\": \"support like AMC/CMC etc., shall not be claimed\
      \ as Local value addition. Such services will\\n              not be considered as ‘Local Content’.\\n\\n\
      \       2.8.   A committee  with  Internal and  external experts  for Independent  verification  of  self-\\\
      n              Declarations by the Bidder may be constituted by BMRCL on random basis and in case of\\n  \
      \            complaints.\\n\\n       2.9.    In case of false declarations by a Bidder claiming as Class-I\
      \ Local Supplier, the following\\n              actions will be taken;\\n\\n                (a) The proposal\
      \ submitted by the Bidder will be rejected.\\n\\n                (b) The Bidder will be debarred for up to\
      \ Two years.\\n\\n       2.10.  Any Bidder from a country which shares a land border with India will be eligible\
      \ to bid in this\\n             Bid only  if the Bidder  is registered with the Competent Authority. Further,\
      \ any bidder\\n               (including from India) having specified Transfer of Technology (ToT) arrangement\
      \ with an\\n                entity from a country which share a land border with India, shall also require\
      \ to be registered\\n              with the same competent authority constituted by Department for Promotion\
      \ of Industry and\\n               Internal Trade (DPIIT), Government of India or the Competent Authority\
      \ constituted by\\n            Government of Karnataka.\\n\\n                          i.  \\\"Bidder\\\"\
      \ (including the term 'Bidder', 'consultant' or 'service provider' in certain contexts)\\n             means\
      \ any person or firm or company, including any member of a consortium or joint\\n                venture (that\
      \ is an association of several persons, or firms or companies), every artificial\\n                    juridical\
      \ person not falling in any of the descriptions of Bidders stated hereinbefore,\\n                  including\
      \ any agency branch or office controlled by such person, participating in a\\n               procurement process.\\\
      n\\n                          ii.  \\\"Bidder from a country which shares a land border with India” means:\\\
      n\\n                    (a) An entity incorporated, established or registered in such a country; or\\n\\n\
      \                    (b) A subsidiary of an entity incorporated, established or registered in such a country;\
      \ or\\n\\n                     (c) An  entity  substantially  controlled through  entities  incorporated,\
      \  established  or\\n                      registered in such a country; or\\n\\n                    (d) An\
      \ entity whose beneficial owner is situated in such a country; or\\n\\n                    (e) An Indian (or\
      \ other) agent of such an entity; or\\n\\n                         (f) A natural person who is a citizen of\
      \ such a country; or\\n\\n                    (g) A consortium or Joint venture where any member of the consortium\
      \ or joint venture\\n                           falls under any of the above.\\n\\n                      \
      \    iii. The beneficial owner for the purpose of (ii) above will be as under:\\n\\n                    (a)\
      \ In case of a company or Limited Liability Partnership, the beneficial owner is the\\n                  \
      \    natural person(s), who, whether acting alone or together, or through one or more\\n                 \
      \         juridical person, has a controlling ownership interest or who exercises control through\\n     \
      \                other means.\\n\\n                         \\\"Controlling ownership interest\\\" means\
      \ ownership of or entitlement to more than\\n                           twenty-five per cent of shares or\
      \ capital or profits of the company;\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 29,\n      \"text\": \"   \\\"\
      Control” shall include the right to appoint majority of the directors or to control\\n                   \
      \      the management or policy decisions including by virtue of their shareholding or\\n                \
      \    management rights or shareholders  agreements  or voting agreements;\\n                    (b) In case\
      \ of a partnership firm, the beneficial owner is the natural person(s) who,\\n                   whether acting\
      \ alone or together, or through one or more juridical person, has\\n                   ownership of entitlement\
      \ to more than fifteen percent of capital or profits of the\\n                      partnership;\\n\\n   \
      \                  (c) In case of an unincorporated association or body of individuals, the beneficial owner\\\
      n                           is the natural person(s), who, whether acting alone or together, or through one\
      \ or\\n                 more juridical person, has ownership of or entitlement to more than fifteen percent\
      \ of\\n                     the property or capital or profits of such association or body of individuals;\\\
      n\\n                    (d) Where no natural person is identified under (a) or (b) or (c) above, the beneficial\\\
      n                  owner is the relevant natural person who holds the position of senior managing\\n     \
      \                       official;\\n\\n                    (e) In case of a trust, the identification  of\
      \ beneficial  owner(s) shall include identification\\n                       of the author of the trust, the\
      \ trustee, the beneficiaries with fifteen percent or more\\n                        interest in the trust\
      \ and any other natural person exercising ultimate effective control\\n                    over the trust\
      \ through a chain of control or ownership.\\n\\n                         (f) An Agent is a person employed\
      \ to do any act for another, or to represent another in\\n                     dealings with third person.\\\
      n\\n                                      i. Successful Bidder shall not be allowed to sub-contract works\
      \ to any contractor from\\n                  a country which shares a land border with India unless such contractor\
      \ is registered\\n                       with the Competent Authority. The definition of “Contractor from\
      \ a country which\\n                     shares a land border with India” shall be as in para (ii) above.\\\
      n                                       ii. The registration shall be valid at the time of submission of bids\
      \ and at the time of\\n                    acceptance of bid.\\n                                       iii.\
      \ If the bidder was validly registered at the time of acceptance / placement of order,\\n                \
      \         registration shall not be relevant consideration during contract execution.\\n       2.11.  Bidder\
      \  is required  to submit undertaking  for compliance  of the above Clause 2.10\\n             (Attachment-1\
      \ to Letter of Technical Bid).\\n\\n       2.12.  The above Clause 2.10 will not apply to Bidders from those\
      \ countries (even if sharing a land\\n             border with India) to which the Government of India has\
      \ extended lines of credit or in which\\n              the Government of India is engaged in development projects.\
      \ Updated lists of countries to\\n             which lines of credit have been extended or in which development\
      \ projects are undertaken\\n             are given in the website of the Ministry of External Affairs.\\n\\\
      n       2.13.  Tenderers shall not have a conflict of interest that affects the Bidding Process. Any Tenderer\\\
      n             found to have a Conflict of Interest shall be disqualified. A Tenderer will be deemed to have\\\
      n               Conflict of interest with one or more parties in this Tendering process, if:\\n\\n       \
      \         (a) A tenderer submits more than one bid either individually or as a member of a JV for a\\n   \
      \             work will result in disqualification of all its bids for that work.\\n\\n                (b)\
      \ The Tenderer has been engaged by the Employer to provide consulting services for the\\n                \
      \  preparation related to procurement for or implementation of this work.\\n\\n                 (c) Any association/affiliation\
      \ (inclusive of parent firms) of the Bidder has been engaged by\\n                  the Employer to provide\
      \ consulting services for the preparation related to procurement\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 30,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"for or implementation of this work.\\n\\n                (d) The Tenderer lends, or temporarily\
      \ seconds its personnel to Firms or organizations\\n                which are engaged in Consulting services\
      \ for the preparation related to procurement for\\n                   or implementation of this work.\\n\\\
      n       2.14.  DELETED\\n\\n       2.15.  As on date of submission of Bid, the Bidder (each member, in case\
      \ of JV) should not be\\n             excluded as per the GROUNDS FOR EXCLUSION. The Bidder should submit\
      \ an\\n              undertaking to this effect.\\n\\n                      (i) GROUNDS FOR EXCLUSION will\
      \ constitute the following, if the Bidders (in case of JV\\n                 applicable to all member(s));\\\
      n\\n                  (a) are bankrupt, being wound up or ceasing their activities, are having their activities\\\
      n                   administered by the courts, have entered into receivership, or are in any analogous\\\
      n                      situation arising from any similar procedure; or\\n\\n                         is under\
      \ insolvency proceedings at the time of bid submission and until the contract is\\n                awarded\
      \ or adjudged insolvent by the National Company Law Tribunal (NCLT), with\\n                 any resolution\
      \ proceedings pending before the NCLT;\\n\\n                  (b) have been convicted within the past five\
      \ years by a court decision, which has the force\\n                      of residential jurisdiction in the\
      \ country where the project is implemented, of fraud or\\n                    corruption or any other offence\
      \ committed during the procurement or performance of a\\n                     contract, unless they provide\
      \ supporting information together with their Statement of\\n                       Integrity which shows that\
      \ this conviction is not relevant in the context of this project;\\n\\n                   (c) have committed\
      \ serious professional misconduct within the past five years during the\\n                 Procurement or\
      \ performance of a contract, as evidenced by any means by the\\n                  Employer;\\n\\n        \
      \          (d) have not fulfilled their obligations regarding the payment of social security contributions\\\
      n                    or taxes in accordance with the legal provisions of the country where they are\\n   \
      \                 established or the Employer ‘s country;\\n\\n                  (e) have indulged in ‘Corrupt\
      \ Practice’; “corrupt practice” means the offering, giving,\\n                    receiving or soliciting\
      \ of anything of value to influence the action of a public official in\\n                   the procurement\
      \ process or in contract execution;\\n\\n                       (f) have indulged in ‘Fraudulent Practice’;\
      \ “fraudulent practice” means a misrepresentation\\n                      of facts in order to influence an\
      \ evaluation process or the execution of a contract, and\\n                    includes misleading or false\
      \ representation in forms or statements or attachment\\n                   submitted and collusive practices\
      \ among Bidders (prior to or after bid submission)\\n                  designed to establish bid prices at\
      \ artificial, non-competitive levels and to deprive the\\n                 Employer of the benefits of free\
      \ and open competitions;\\n\\n                  (g) have been considered ineligible due to execution of Bid–Securing\
      \ Declaration by any\\n                    Central Govt./State Govt./PSUs;\\n\\n                  (h) have\
      \ been Black  listed/debarred /banned business by any Central Govt./State\\n                 Govt./PSUs or\
      \ Local Authority or World Bank or ADB or JICA or any other International\\n                   Financing Institution,\
      \ or any entity controlled by them;\\n\\n                         (i) have left/abandoned the awarded works\
      \ on his own in the last 05 years;\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 31,\n      \"text\": \"(j) “have\
      \ been rescinded / terminated by any Central Govt. or State Govt. or PSUs or\\n                Government\
      \ entity after award, during last 5 years”;\\n\\n                   (k) have been either penalized with liquidated\
      \ damages of 5% (or more) of the contract\\n                   value due to delay or imposed with penalty\
      \ of 5% (or more) of the contract value due\\n                     to any other reason in any Contract awarded\
      \ by BMRCL/ any other Metro Organization\\n                (100% owned by Govt.), during last three years\
      \ (from the day of bid submission);\\n\\n                       (ii) Bidders may also note that in case the\
      \ Bidder is excluded as per Ground for Exclusion\\n               mentioned above, after the due date of submission\
      \ of Bid but before award of the contract\\n               by BMRCL, they shall inform the same to BMRCL in\
      \ writing within 5 working days, failing\\n               which it will be considered that the Bidder has\
      \ wilfully concealed the information and the\\n                Bidder shall be solely responsible for all\
      \ implications that may arise in accordance with\\n                 the conditions of this Bid.\\n\\n    \
      \                    (iii) Any such exclusion will result in disqualification of the Bidder and the financial\
      \ proposal\\n                   of such Bidder shall not be opened in case the technical evaluation is still\
      \ in progress. In\\n               case the Financial Proposals have already been opened, and the work not\
      \ yet awarded,\\n                 the Financial Proposal will not be further considered for evaluation.\"\n\
      \    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Eligible Tenderers\"\n}"

  text: Where completion certificates are inclusive of taxes, tenderers must deduct applicable GST or a pre-period
    tax rate of 12% supported by a Chartered Accountant certification.

  relation_type: none

    quote: "{\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"4) \\nIn cases where the Client’s\
      \ Completion Certificate indicates values inclusive of GST and/or other applicable taxes, the Tenderer shall\
      \ \\nsubmit the actual completion cost after deducting the applicable GST dully certified by Chartered Accountant.\
      \ For portions of Similar \\nNature Works executed in the pre-GST period, the Tenderer shall make a deduction\
      \ towards taxes at the rate of 12% to arrive at the \\ncompletion cost exclusive of taxes.\"\n    }\n  ],\n\
      \  \"note_id\": \"work_experience_note_4\",\n  \"number\": 4,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
      \n}"

      \ \"note\": \"Source text is preserved. Individual rows, nested milestone tables and their relationships have\

    from the issue of the Letter of Acceptance.

    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 88,\n  \"text\": \"Protection   of  4.17       The Contractor shall take all reasonable\
      \ steps to protect the environment (both on\\n     the                       and off the Site) and to avoid\
      \ injury, damage and nuisance to people and property\\n    Environment                   resulting from pollution,\
      \ noise and other results of his operations. The Contractor\\n                                        shall\
      \ ensure that air emissions, surface discharges and effluent from the Site\\n                            \
      \        during the Contract Period shall not exceed the values indicated in the Employer's\\n           \
      \                     Requirements & Technical  specifications, and  shall not exceed the values\\n      \
      \                              prescribed by law. The Contractor shall conform to the Employer’s Requirements\\\
      n                         & Technical specifications and shall indemnify the Employer against any liability\\\
      n                                     or damages or claims arising out of his operations. The Contractor shall\
      \ be\\n                                    responsible and liable for any stoppage, closure or suspension\
      \ of the works due\\n                                        to any contravention of statutory requirements\
      \ relating to the protection of the\\n                                 environment and shall indemnify and\
      \ keep indemnified the Employer in this\\n                                     regard.\\n                \
      \                     Outline Environmental Plan means the environmental plan forming part of the\\n     \
      \                             Tender, setting out,  in summary form, the Contractor's proposed means of\\\
      n                                 complying  with  his  obligations  in  relation  to  environmental  quality.\
      \  Site\\n                                  Environmental Plan means the site environmental plan including\
      \ all supplements\\n                                       thereto, or any amended or varied version thereof,\
      \ as submitted by the Contractor\\n                                           in accordance with Employer’s\
      \ Safety, Health and Environmental Manual (SHE\\n                                  Manual), this Clause and\
      \ which has received the Engineer’s consent. The Site\\n                                  Environmental Plan\
      \ shall include detailed policies, procedures and regulations\\n                                   which,\
      \ when implemented,  will ensure compliance  with  this  Clause. The\\n                                  \
      \  Contractor is required to make himself aware of  all the requirements of the\\n                       \
      \          Employer’s SHE Manual in this regard and comply with them.\\n\\n                             The\
      \ Contractor's Site Environmental Plan shall be developed from his Employer’s\\n                         \
      \            Safety. Health and Environmental Manual (SHE Manual), as per the Employer's\\n              \
      \                   Requirements.\\n\\n                       4.17.1      Within stipulated time as mentioned\
      \ in ‘contract data’, the Contractor shall submit\\n                              a detailed and comprehensive\
      \ Site Environmental Plan based on the Employer’s\\n                                     Safety, Health and\
      \ Environmental Manual (SHE Manual), and shall include such\\n                                        further\
      \ material, which the Contractor considers necessary and relevant.\\n\\n                            Upon the\
      \ Engineer notifying his consent to the Site Environmental Plan, or any\\n                               \
      \  supplemental part thereof, the Contractor shall adhere to the principles and\\n                       \
      \          procedures contained in such document save to the extent that the Engineer may\\n             \
      \                       give his consent to any amended or varied version thereof.\\n                    \
      \         The Contractor shall provide all necessary access, assistance and facilities to\\n             \
      \                    enable the Engineer and the Employer to monitor and conduct tests to verify that\\n \
      \                                   the Site Environmental Plan is being properly and fully implemented.\\\
      n\\n      Electricity,       4.18      The Contractor shall be responsible for making his own arrangements\
      \ at his own\\n    Water and Gas                cost to obtain supply of water, electricity or gas for the\
      \ Works. The Employer where\\n                                       feasible may at his discretion assist\
      \ the Contractor in giving recommendatory\\n                                           letters etc.\\n   \
      \  Tools, Plants     4.19       Except for any specific item mentioned in the Special Conditions of Contract\
      \ or in\\n    And Equipment              Employer’s Requirements, the Contractor shall provide  all tools,\
      \ plants and\\n    Supplied By                Equipment  for the Works.  In respect  of such exceptional \
      \ tools, plants  or\\n    The Employer              Equipment committed to be provided by the Employer under\
      \ terms and conditions\\n                                      specified in the Special Conditions of Contract,\
      \ the Contractor shall take  all\\n                                  reasonable care and shall be responsible\
      \ for all damages or loss caused by him,\\n                                       his representatives, sub-contractors\
      \ or his workmen or others while they are in his\\n                                   charge.\\n         \
      \                 No  tools, plant and equipment shall be supplied by the Employer. Unless\\n            \
      \                             specifically incorporated in special conditions, the Contractor has to arrange\
      \ all\\n                                          tools, plant, equipment required for the work.\\n      \
      \                   On completion of the Works, the Contractor shall hand over the unused balance\\n     \
      \                                   of the tools, plants and Equipment supplied by the Employer to the Employer\
      \ back\\n                                           in good order and repair, fair wear and tear expected,\
      \ and shall be responsible for\\n                               any failure to account for the same or any\
      \ damage done thereto.\\n                             The decision of the Engineer as to the amount recoverable\
      \ from the Contractor on\\n                                            this account shall be final and binding.\"\
      \n}"

- kind: summary
  text: Claims for additional payment must be notified to the Engineer as soon as possible and within 28 days of
    the start of the event giving rise to the claim, followed by a fully detailed claim within 28 days of notice.
  evidence_ids:
  - J3808711ea48ee2c8
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C4f0d02634b92ea72
  citations:
  - id: J3808711ea48ee2c8
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secE.md
    okf_sha256: d72224b0c08c509f8485b0d971bd10c690c5dace89c225ac4a8fcb0f9f2c9dad
    heading: Clause 17 — Claims, Disputes and Conciliation
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 139
    - 140
    - 141
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_e_gcc/gcc_clauses.json
    json_sha256: ebcddb3df6158a35353d5cbabda41eaabf5b49ea6a2e275040396356122c49d5
    json_pointer: /clauses/16
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"17\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 139,\n      \"text\": \"\
      17         Claims, Disputes and Conciliation\\n                         Any and all Disputes shall be settled\
      \ in accordance with the provision of this\\n                           Clause\\nProcedure for     17.1  \
      \           If the Contractor intends to claim any additional payment under any clause of\\nClaims       \
      \               these Conditions or otherwise, the Contractor shall give notice to the Engineer as\\n    \
      \                      soon as possible and in any event within 28 days of the start of the event giving\\\
      n                                   rise to the claim.\\n\\n                         The Contractor shall\
      \ keep such contemporary records as may be necessary to\\n                                substantiate any\
      \ claim, either on the Site or at any other location acceptable to the\\n                              Engineer.\
      \ Without admitting the Employer’s liability, the Engineer shall, on receipt\\n                          \
      \        of such notice, inspect such records and may instruct the Contractor to keep\\n                 \
      \                 further contemporary records. The Contractor shall permit the Engineer to inspect\\n   \
      \                                        all such records, and shall (if instructed) submit copies to the\
      \ Engineer.\\n\\n\\n                               Within 28 days of such notice, or such other time as may\
      \ be agreed by the\\n                              Engineer, the Contractor shall send to the Engineer a fully\
      \ detailed claim which\\n                               includes full supporting particulars of the basis\
      \ of the claim and additional payment\\n                               claimed. If the event or circumstance\
      \ giving rise to the claim has a continuing\\n                                    effect:\\n\\n          \
      \                        a.  the initial fully detailed claim shall be considered as interim;\\n\\n      \
      \                            b.  the Contractor shall send further interim claims at monthly intervals, giving\
      \ the\\n                                  cumulative amount claimed, and any such further particulars as the\
      \ Engineer\\n                         may reasonably require; and\\n\\n                                  \
      \  c.  the contractor shall send a final claim within 28 days after the end of effects\\n                \
      \                      resulting from the event or circumstance, or within such other period as may\\n   \
      \                          be proposed by the Contractor and approved by the Engineer.\\n\\n             \
      \                                      If the Contractor fails to comply with this Sub-clause, he shall not\
      \ be entitled to\\n                               claim any additional payment.\\n\\nPayment for      17.2\
      \      The Contractor  shall be  entitled  to have included  in any Interim Payment\\nClaims             \
      \              Certificate such amount for any claim as the Engineer considers due, after taking\\n      \
      \                        approval from  the Employer.  If the  particulars supplied are  insufficient  to\\\
      n                                substantiate the whole of the claim, the Contractor shall be entitled to\
      \ payment for\\n                           such part of the claim as has been substantiated.\\n\\nNo Legal\
      \         17.3      Any and all Disputes shall be settled in accordance with the provisions of Clause\\naction\
      \ till                     17. No action at law concerning or arising out of any Dispute shall be commenced\\\
      nDispute                      unless and until all applicable Dispute resolution procedures set out in Clause\
      \ 17\\nSettlement                      shall have been finally exhausted in relation to that Dispute or any\
      \ Dispute out of\\nProcedure is                which that Dispute shall have arisen with which  it may be\
      \ or may have been\\nExhausted                   connected.\\n\\nNotice of         17.4     A Dispute shall\
      \ be deemed to arise when one party serves a notice in writing\\nDispute                         (hereinafter\
      \ called a \\\"Notice of Dispute\\\") within 30 days from the date of rejection\\n                       \
      \           of claim by the parties.\\n\\n\\nDispute           17.5       Disputes shall be settled through\
      \ Conciliation procedures as established by “The\\nResolution                      Arbitration and Conciliation\
      \ Act, 1996” and as amended from time to time.\\n\\nConciliation      17.6        Within 60 days of receipt\
      \ of Notice of Dispute, either party shall refer the matter in\\n                               dispute to\
      \ conciliation. Conciliation proceedings shall be initiated within 30 days\\n                            \
      \      of one party  inviting the other  in  writing  to  Conciliation.  Conciliation  shall\\n          \
      \             commence when the other party accepts in writing this invitation.  If the invitation\\n    \
      \                                    is not accepted, then Conciliation shall not take place.    If the party\
      \ initiating\\n                                   conciliation does not receive a reply within 30 days from\
      \ the date on which he\\n                           sends the invitation, he may elect to treat this as a\
      \ rejection of the invitation to\\n                                  conciliate and inform the other party\
      \ accordingly.\\n                         The Conciliation shall be undertaken by one Conciliator selected\
      \ from a panel of\\n                                 Conciliators maintained by the Employer. The Conciliator\
      \ shall assist the parties\\n                                  to reach an amicable settlement in an independent\
      \ and impartial manner.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 140,\n      \"text\": \"Conciliation      17.7\
      \      The Employer shall maintain a panel of Conciliators with requisite qualifications\\n    Procedure \
      \                and professional experience who shall be from serving or retired engineers of\\n        \
      \                      Government Departments, or of Public Sector Undertakings. Panel  list not to\\n   \
      \                                  include serving or former employees of the Employer. Out of this panel,\
      \ a list of\\n                                    three Conciliators shall be sent to the Contractor who shall\
      \ choose one of them to\\n                                     act as Conciliator and conduct conciliation\
      \ proceedings in accordance with “The\\n                                        Arbitration and Conciliation\
      \ Act, 1996”, of India. The party serving notice of dispute\\n                              on the other party\
      \ shall also serve such notice on the Conciliator chosen as per\\n                                       \
      \    this Clause. The Employer at the time of offering the panel of Conciliators to be\\n                \
      \                  appointed as Conciliator shall also supply the information with regard to the\\n      \
      \                                  qualifications of the said Conciliators nominated in the panel along with\
      \ their\\n                                     professional experience, phone nos. and addresses to the Contractor.\\\
      n\\n                                        In the event of the Contractor not choosing the Conciliator from\
      \ the panel furnished\\n                               by the Employer, within a period of 30 days, the Employer\
      \ is at liberty to choose\\n                              and nominate a Conciliator from the same panel and\
      \ communicate to the\\n                                    Contractor which will be final and binding on the\
      \ Contractor.\\n\\n\\n                             The Employer and the Contractor  shall  in good  faith\
      \ co-operate  with the\\n                                       Conciliator and, in particular, shall endeavour\
      \ to comply with requests by the\\n                                       Conciliator to submit written materials,\
      \ provide evidence and attend meetings.\\n                             Each party may, on his own initiative\
      \ or at the invitation of the Conciliator, submit\\n                                        to the Conciliator\
      \ suggestions for the settlement of the dispute.\\n\\n\\n                         When it appears to the Conciliator\
      \ that there exist elements of a settlement which\\n                          may be acceptable to the parties,\
      \ he shall formulate the terms of a possible\\n                                    settlement and submit them\
      \ to the parties for their observations. After receiving\\n                                    the observations\
      \ of the parties, the Conciliator may reformulate the terms of a\\n                                    possible\
      \ settlement in the light of such observations.\\n\\n\\n                                                 \
      \           If the Parties reach agreement on a settlement of the dispute, they may draw up\\n           \
      \                   and sign a  written settlement agreement.  If requested by the Parties, the\\n       \
      \                                Conciliator may draw up, or assist the Parties in drawing up, the settlement\\\
      n                                 agreement.\\n                         When the Parties sign the settlement\
      \ agreement, it shall be final and binding on\\n                                    the parties and persons\
      \ claiming under them respectively.\\n                             The Conciliator shall authenticate the\
      \ settlement agreement and furnish a copy\\n                                     thereof to each of the Parties.\\\
      n\\n\\n                            To the fullest extent feasible, the conciliatory process must be concluded\
      \ within a\\n\\n                                    period of sixty days subsequent to the acknowledgment\
      \ of the appointment notice\\n\\n                               by the Conciliator.\\n\\n                \
      \             The Parties shall not initiate, during the conciliation proceedings, any judicial\\n       \
      \                           proceedings in respect of a dispute that is the subject matter of the conciliation\\\
      n                                   proceedings.\\n\\n    Termination of    17.8      The conciliation proceedings\
      \ shall be terminated:\\n     Conciliation                    a.  by the signing of the settlement agreement\
      \ by the Parties on the date of\\n    Proceedings                    agreement; or\\n                    \
      \                    b.  by written declaration of the Conciliator, after consultation with the Parties, to\\\
      n                                        the effect further efforts at conciliation are no longer justified,\
      \ on the date of\\n                                           declaration; or\\n                         \
      \                 c.  by a written declaration of the Parties to the Conciliator to the effect that the\\\
      n                                             conciliation proceedings are terminated, on the date of declaration;\
      \ or\\n                                        d.  by a written declaration of a Party to the other Party\
      \ and the Conciliator,  if\\n                                       appointed, to the effect that the conciliation\
      \ proceedings are terminated, on\\n                                        the date of declaration.\\n   \
      \                         Upon termination of the conciliation proceedings, the Conciliator shall fix the\
      \ costs\\n                                        of the conciliation and give written notice thereof to the\
      \ Parties. The costs shall be\\n                                borne equally by the Parties unless settlement\
      \ agreement provides for a different\\n                                   apportionment. All other expenses\
      \ incurred by a Party shall be borne by that Party.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 141,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"Suspension of   17.9       The reference to Conciliation shall proceed not withstanding that the Works\
      \ shall\\nWork on                      not then be or be alleged to be complete, provided always that the\
      \ obligations of\\nAccount of                    the Employer, Engineer and the Contractor shall not be altered\
      \ by reasons of\\nconciliation                     conciliation being conducted during the progress of the\
      \ Works. Neither party shall\\n                          be entitled to suspend the work or part of the work\
      \ to which the dispute relates on\\n                            account of conciliation and payments to the\
      \ Contractor shall continue to be made\\n                                     in terms of the Contract.\\\
      n\\nJudicial          17.10        In the event that a dispute remains unresolved via the conciliation process,\
      \ the\\nRecourses                   aggrieved party may seek judicial recourses.\\n\\nJurisdiction      17.11\
      \      Any suit or application, arising out of any dispute or differences on account of this\\nof Courts \
      \                     contract shall be filed in a competent court at Bangalore, Karnataka only and no\\n\
      \                               other court or any other district of the country shall have any jurisdiction\
      \ on the\\n                                matter.\\n\\nMatters finally    17.12            If the Contractor\
      \ (s) does/do not prefer his/ their specific and final claims in writing,\\ndetermined by                \
      \ within a period of 90 days of receiving the intimation from the Employer that the\\nthe Employer       \

- kind: summary
  text: Disputes must first be referred to conciliation within 60 days of receipt of a Notice of Dispute under GCC
    Clause 17 before initiating any judicial recourse or action at law.
  evidence_ids:
  - J3808711ea48ee2c8
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation

  to_id: ''

  - id: J3808711ea48ee2c8
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secE.md
    okf_sha256: d72224b0c08c509f8485b0d971bd10c690c5dace89c225ac4a8fcb0f9f2c9dad
    heading: Clause 17 — Claims, Disputes and Conciliation
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c

    json_sha256: ebcddb3df6158a35353d5cbabda41eaabf5b49ea6a2e275040396356122c49d5
    json_pointer: /clauses/16

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"17\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 139,\n      \"text\": \"\
      17         Claims, Disputes and Conciliation\\n                         Any and all Disputes shall be settled\
      \ in accordance with the provision of this\\n                           Clause\\nProcedure for     17.1  \
      \           If the Contractor intends to claim any additional payment under any clause of\\nClaims       \
      \               these Conditions or otherwise, the Contractor shall give notice to the Engineer as\\n    \
      \                      soon as possible and in any event within 28 days of the start of the event giving\\\
      n                                   rise to the claim.\\n\\n                         The Contractor shall\
      \ keep such contemporary records as may be necessary to\\n                                substantiate any\
      \ claim, either on the Site or at any other location acceptable to the\\n                              Engineer.\
      \ Without admitting the Employer’s liability, the Engineer shall, on receipt\\n                          \
      \        of such notice, inspect such records and may instruct the Contractor to keep\\n                 \
      \                 further contemporary records. The Contractor shall permit the Engineer to inspect\\n   \
      \                                        all such records, and shall (if instructed) submit copies to the\
      \ Engineer.\\n\\n\\n                               Within 28 days of such notice, or such other time as may\
      \ be agreed by the\\n                              Engineer, the Contractor shall send to the Engineer a fully\
      \ detailed claim which\\n                               includes full supporting particulars of the basis\
      \ of the claim and additional payment\\n                               claimed. If the event or circumstance\
      \ giving rise to the claim has a continuing\\n                                    effect:\\n\\n          \
      \                        a.  the initial fully detailed claim shall be considered as interim;\\n\\n      \
      \                            b.  the Contractor shall send further interim claims at monthly intervals, giving\
      \ the\\n                                  cumulative amount claimed, and any such further particulars as the\
      \ Engineer\\n                         may reasonably require; and\\n\\n                                  \
      \  c.  the contractor shall send a final claim within 28 days after the end of effects\\n                \
      \                      resulting from the event or circumstance, or within such other period as may\\n   \
      \                          be proposed by the Contractor and approved by the Engineer.\\n\\n             \
      \                                      If the Contractor fails to comply with this Sub-clause, he shall not\
      \ be entitled to\\n                               claim any additional payment.\\n\\nPayment for      17.2\
      \      The Contractor  shall be  entitled  to have included  in any Interim Payment\\nClaims             \
      \              Certificate such amount for any claim as the Engineer considers due, after taking\\n      \
      \                        approval from  the Employer.  If the  particulars supplied are  insufficient  to\\\
      n                                substantiate the whole of the claim, the Contractor shall be entitled to\
      \ payment for\\n                           such part of the claim as has been substantiated.\\n\\nNo Legal\
      \         17.3      Any and all Disputes shall be settled in accordance with the provisions of Clause\\naction\
      \ till                     17. No action at law concerning or arising out of any Dispute shall be commenced\\\
      nDispute                      unless and until all applicable Dispute resolution procedures set out in Clause\
      \ 17\\nSettlement                      shall have been finally exhausted in relation to that Dispute or any\
      \ Dispute out of\\nProcedure is                which that Dispute shall have arisen with which  it may be\
      \ or may have been\\nExhausted                   connected.\\n\\nNotice of         17.4     A Dispute shall\
      \ be deemed to arise when one party serves a notice in writing\\nDispute                         (hereinafter\
      \ called a \\\"Notice of Dispute\\\") within 30 days from the date of rejection\\n                       \
      \           of claim by the parties.\\n\\n\\nDispute           17.5       Disputes shall be settled through\
      \ Conciliation procedures as established by “The\\nResolution                      Arbitration and Conciliation\
      \ Act, 1996” and as amended from time to time.\\n\\nConciliation      17.6        Within 60 days of receipt\
      \ of Notice of Dispute, either party shall refer the matter in\\n                               dispute to\
      \ conciliation. Conciliation proceedings shall be initiated within 30 days\\n                            \
      \      of one party  inviting the other  in  writing  to  Conciliation.  Conciliation  shall\\n          \
      \             commence when the other party accepts in writing this invitation.  If the invitation\\n    \
      \                                    is not accepted, then Conciliation shall not take place.    If the party\
      \ initiating\\n                                   conciliation does not receive a reply within 30 days from\
      \ the date on which he\\n                           sends the invitation, he may elect to treat this as a\
      \ rejection of the invitation to\\n                                  conciliate and inform the other party\
      \ accordingly.\\n                         The Conciliation shall be undertaken by one Conciliator selected\
      \ from a panel of\\n                                 Conciliators maintained by the Employer. The Conciliator\
      \ shall assist the parties\\n                                  to reach an amicable settlement in an independent\
      \ and impartial manner.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 140,\n      \"text\": \"Conciliation      17.7\
      \      The Employer shall maintain a panel of Conciliators with requisite qualifications\\n    Procedure \
      \                and professional experience who shall be from serving or retired engineers of\\n        \
      \                      Government Departments, or of Public Sector Undertakings. Panel  list not to\\n   \
      \                                  include serving or former employees of the Employer. Out of this panel,\
      \ a list of\\n                                    three Conciliators shall be sent to the Contractor who shall\
      \ choose one of them to\\n                                     act as Conciliator and conduct conciliation\
      \ proceedings in accordance with “The\\n                                        Arbitration and Conciliation\
      \ Act, 1996”, of India. The party serving notice of dispute\\n                              on the other party\
      \ shall also serve such notice on the Conciliator chosen as per\\n                                       \
      \    this Clause. The Employer at the time of offering the panel of Conciliators to be\\n                \
      \                  appointed as Conciliator shall also supply the information with regard to the\\n      \
      \                                  qualifications of the said Conciliators nominated in the panel along with\
      \ their\\n                                     professional experience, phone nos. and addresses to the Contractor.\\\
      n\\n                                        In the event of the Contractor not choosing the Conciliator from\
      \ the panel furnished\\n                               by the Employer, within a period of 30 days, the Employer\
      \ is at liberty to choose\\n                              and nominate a Conciliator from the same panel and\
      \ communicate to the\\n                                    Contractor which will be final and binding on the\
      \ Contractor.\\n\\n\\n                             The Employer and the Contractor  shall  in good  faith\
      \ co-operate  with the\\n                                       Conciliator and, in particular, shall endeavour\
      \ to comply with requests by the\\n                                       Conciliator to submit written materials,\
      \ provide evidence and attend meetings.\\n                             Each party may, on his own initiative\
      \ or at the invitation of the Conciliator, submit\\n                                        to the Conciliator\
      \ suggestions for the settlement of the dispute.\\n\\n\\n                         When it appears to the Conciliator\
      \ that there exist elements of a settlement which\\n                          may be acceptable to the parties,\
      \ he shall formulate the terms of a possible\\n                                    settlement and submit them\
      \ to the parties for their observations. After receiving\\n                                    the observations\
      \ of the parties, the Conciliator may reformulate the terms of a\\n                                    possible\
      \ settlement in the light of such observations.\\n\\n\\n                                                 \
      \           If the Parties reach agreement on a settlement of the dispute, they may draw up\\n           \
      \                   and sign a  written settlement agreement.  If requested by the Parties, the\\n       \
      \                                Conciliator may draw up, or assist the Parties in drawing up, the settlement\\\
      n                                 agreement.\\n                         When the Parties sign the settlement\
      \ agreement, it shall be final and binding on\\n                                    the parties and persons\
      \ claiming under them respectively.\\n                             The Conciliator shall authenticate the\
      \ settlement agreement and furnish a copy\\n                                     thereof to each of the Parties.\\\
      n\\n\\n                            To the fullest extent feasible, the conciliatory process must be concluded\
      \ within a\\n\\n                                    period of sixty days subsequent to the acknowledgment\
      \ of the appointment notice\\n\\n                               by the Conciliator.\\n\\n                \
      \             The Parties shall not initiate, during the conciliation proceedings, any judicial\\n       \
      \                           proceedings in respect of a dispute that is the subject matter of the conciliation\\\
      n                                   proceedings.\\n\\n    Termination of    17.8      The conciliation proceedings\
      \ shall be terminated:\\n     Conciliation                    a.  by the signing of the settlement agreement\
      \ by the Parties on the date of\\n    Proceedings                    agreement; or\\n                    \
      \                    b.  by written declaration of the Conciliator, after consultation with the Parties, to\\\
      n                                        the effect further efforts at conciliation are no longer justified,\
      \ on the date of\\n                                           declaration; or\\n                         \
      \                 c.  by a written declaration of the Parties to the Conciliator to the effect that the\\\
      n                                             conciliation proceedings are terminated, on the date of declaration;\
      \ or\\n                                        d.  by a written declaration of a Party to the other Party\
      \ and the Conciliator,  if\\n                                       appointed, to the effect that the conciliation\
      \ proceedings are terminated, on\\n                                        the date of declaration.\\n   \
      \                         Upon termination of the conciliation proceedings, the Conciliator shall fix the\
      \ costs\\n                                        of the conciliation and give written notice thereof to the\
      \ Parties. The costs shall be\\n                                borne equally by the Parties unless settlement\
      \ agreement provides for a different\\n                                   apportionment. All other expenses\
      \ incurred by a Party shall be borne by that Party.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 141,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"Suspension of   17.9       The reference to Conciliation shall proceed not withstanding that the Works\
      \ shall\\nWork on                      not then be or be alleged to be complete, provided always that the\
      \ obligations of\\nAccount of                    the Employer, Engineer and the Contractor shall not be altered\
      \ by reasons of\\nconciliation                     conciliation being conducted during the progress of the\
      \ Works. Neither party shall\\n                          be entitled to suspend the work or part of the work\
      \ to which the dispute relates on\\n                            account of conciliation and payments to the\
      \ Contractor shall continue to be made\\n                                     in terms of the Contract.\\\
      n\\nJudicial          17.10        In the event that a dispute remains unresolved via the conciliation process,\
      \ the\\nRecourses                   aggrieved party may seek judicial recourses.\\n\\nJurisdiction      17.11\
      \      Any suit or application, arising out of any dispute or differences on account of this\\nof Courts \
      \                     contract shall be filed in a competent court at Bangalore, Karnataka only and no\\n\
      \                               other court or any other district of the country shall have any jurisdiction\
      \ on the\\n                                matter.\\n\\nMatters finally    17.12            If the Contractor\
      \ (s) does/do not prefer his/ their specific and final claims in writing,\\ndetermined by                \
      \ within a period of 90 days of receiving the intimation from the Employer that the\\nthe Employer       \
      \               final bill is ready for payment, he/they will be deemed to have waived his/their\\n      \
      \                        claims (s) and the Employer shall be discharged and released of all liabilities under\\\
      n                               the contract in respect of these claims.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\
      : \"17         Claims, Disputes and Conciliation\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"\
      title\": \"Claims, Disputes and Conciliation\"\n}"

    heading: Clause 4.0 — Contractor SHE Policy and Plan
    source_document: Vol3DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 1ee3fc8dfd12f659f34446b0a6db74e1e1cdd4d593ca91b2a48d59bf1ef97d52

    json_path: parsed_json/vol3/part_1_she_management/she_management_clauses.json
    json_sha256: b017881487993483cddcb5c17ffd7e2c6898a21f9463d8fab51b9e05d5e0a9df
    json_pointer: /clauses/3
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"4.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"\
      4.0      Contractor SHE Policy and Plan\\n\\n          4.1     The contractor as per Section 39 of the BOCW\
      \ Act shall formulate a SHE policy and\\n                    get it approved by DG respectively and display\
      \ it at conspicuous places at work sites\\n                        in Kannada, Hindi and other languages understood\
      \ by the majority of construction\\n                   workers.\\n\\n\\n          4.2       Within 4 weeks\
      \ of the notification of acceptance of the tender, the Contractor shall\\n                  submit a detailed\
      \ and comprehensive Contract specific SHE Plan. The SHE Plan shall\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"include detailed policies, procedures and regulations which, when implemented, will\\n\
      \         ensure compliance  of the contract  provisions. The SHE Plan  shall include the\\n          following\
      \ but not be restricted to:\\n\\n\\n                i)   A statement of the Contractor’s policy, organisation\
      \ and arrangements for SHE\\n\\n                  ii)   The name(s) and experience of person(s) within the\
      \ Contractor’s proposed\\n            management who shall be responsible for co-ordinating and monitoring\
      \ the\\n                Contractor’s SHE performance;\\n\\n                  iii)   The number  of SHE  staff\
      \ who  shall be employed on  the Works,  their\\n                  responsibilities,  authority  and  line\
      \  of  communication  with  the  proposed\\n                Contractor's agent;\\n\\n             iv)   A\
      \ statement  of the Contractor’s policy and procedures  for  identifying and\\n                estimating\
      \ hazards, and the measures for addressing the same;\\n\\n           v)   A list of SHE hazards anticipated\
      \ for this Contract and sufficient information to\\n              demonstrate the Contractor’s proposals for\
      \ achieving effective and  efficient\\n                health and safety procedures;\\n\\n             vi)\
      \   A description of the SHE training courses and emergency drills which shall\\n\\n             be provided\
      \ by the Contractor, with an outline of the syllabus to be followed;\\n\\n              vii)   Details of\
      \ the safety equipment which shall be provided by the Contractor,\\n                including personal protective\
      \ equipment;\\n\\n               viii)  A statement of the Contractor’s policy and procedures for ensuring\
      \ that\\n                Contractor's Equipment used on the Project Site are maintained in a safe\\n     \
      \           condition and are operated in a safe manner;\\n\\n             ix)  A statement of the Contractor’s\
      \ policy and procedures for ensuring that sub-\\n                contractors comply with the Contractor's\
      \ safety plan;\\n\\n           x)   A statement of the Contractor’s disciplinary procedures with respect to\
      \ SHE\\n                 related matters, and\\n\\n             xi)  A statement of the Contractor’s procedure\
      \ for reporting and investigating\\n                accidents, dangerous occurrences or occupational illnesses\\\
      n\\n\\n4.3     The Contractor shall, from time to time and as necessary are required by the\\n         Employer\
      \ to produce supplements to the SHE Plan such that  it is at all times a\\n           detailed, comprehensive\
      \ and contemporaneous statement by the Contractor of his\\n            site safety, industrial health and\
      \ environment obligations, responsibilities, policies\\n        and procedures relating to work on Site. Any\
      \ and all submissions of supplements\\n           to the SHE Plan shall be made to the Employer in accordance\
      \ with the agreed\\n         procedures.\\n\\n4.4           If at any time the SHE plan is, in the Employer’s\
      \ opinion, insufficient or requires\\n           revision or modification to ensure the security of the Works\
      \ and the safety of all\\n        workmen upon and visitors to the Site, the Employer may instruct the Contractor\\\
      n           to revise the SHE plan and the Contractor shall within 7 days submit the revised\\n          plan\
      \ to the Employer for review.\\n\\n\\n4.5     Any omissions, inconsistencies and errors in the SHE Plan or\
      \ the Employer’s\\n         acceptance or rejection of the SHE Plan and/or supplements thereto shall be\\\
      n          without prejudice  to the Contractor's  obligations with respect  to  site  safety,\\n        \
      \   industrial health and environment and  shall not excuse any  failure by the\\n          contractor  to\
      \ adopt proper and recognised  safety  practices throughout  the\\n          execution of the Work.\\n\\n\\\
      n4.6     The Contractor  shall adhere  to the SHE Plan and  shall ensure, as  far as\\n           practically\
      \ possible, that  all sub-contractors of  all tiers require that contracting\\n           parties each have\
      \ a copy of the Site SHE Plan and comply with its provisions.\\n\\n\\n4.7     The details of contents to be\
      \ covered in the site SHE plan are given in Appendix\\n         No.: 3.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\
      : \"          4.0      Contractor SHE Policy and Plan\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\

      \  Details of the safety equipment which shall be provided by the Contractor,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 30,\n      \"text\": \"                including personal protective\
      \ equipment;\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 31,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"    \
      \           viii)  A statement of the Contractor’s policy and procedures for ensuring that\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"                Contractor's\
      \ Equipment used on the Project Site are maintained in a safe\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"                condition and are operated in a safe\
      \ manner;\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"        \
      \     ix)  A statement of the Contractor’s policy and procedures for ensuring that sub-\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 37,\n      \"text\": \"                contractors\
      \ comply with the Contractor's safety plan;\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 38,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 39,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"           x)   A statement of the Contractor’s disciplinary procedures with respect to\
      \ SHE\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"   \
      \              related matters, and\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 41,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 42,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"             xi)  A statement of the Contractor’s procedure for reporting and investigating\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 43,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \      accidents, dangerous occurrences or occupational illnesses\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n\
      \      \"source_line_number\": 44,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 45,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 46,\n      \"text\": \"4.3     The Contractor shall, from time to time and as necessary are required by\
      \ the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 47,\n      \"text\": \"   \
      \      Employer to produce supplements to the SHE Plan such that  it is at all times a\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 48,\n      \"text\": \"           detailed, comprehensive\
      \ and contemporaneous statement by the Contractor of his\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n     \
      \ \"source_line_number\": 49,\n      \"text\": \"            site safety, industrial health and environment\
      \ obligations, responsibilities, policies\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 50,\n      \"text\": \"        and procedures relating to work on Site. Any and all submissions of supplements\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 51,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \ to the SHE Plan shall be made to the Employer in accordance with the agreed\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 52,\n      \"text\": \"         procedures.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 53,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"4.4           If at any time the SHE plan is,\
      \ in the Employer’s opinion, insufficient or requires\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 55,\n      \"text\": \"           revision or modification to ensure the security of\
      \ the Works and the safety of all\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 56,\n      \"text\": \"        workmen upon and visitors to the Site, the Employer may instruct the Contractor\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 57,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \ to revise the SHE plan and the Contractor shall within 7 days submit the revised\"\n    },\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 58,\n      \"text\": \"          plan to the Employer\
      \ for review.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 59,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 60,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 61,\n      \"text\": \"4.5     Any\
      \ omissions, inconsistencies and errors in the SHE Plan or the Employer’s\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 62,\n      \"text\": \"         acceptance or rejection of the SHE Plan\
      \ and/or supplements thereto shall be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 63,\n      \"text\": \"          without prejudice  to the Contractor's  obligations with respect  to  site\
      \  safety,\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 64,\n      \"text\": \"\
      \           industrial health and environment and  shall not excuse any  failure by the\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 65,\n      \"text\": \"          contractor  to adopt\
      \ proper and recognised  safety  practices throughout  the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n   \
      \   \"source_line_number\": 66,\n      \"text\": \"          execution of the Work.\"\n    },\n    {\n   \
      \   \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 67,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 68,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 69,\n      \"text\": \"4.6     The Contractor  shall adhere  to the SHE Plan\
      \ and  shall ensure, as  far as\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 70,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"           practically possible, that  all sub-contractors of  all tiers require that contracting\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 71,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \ parties each have a copy of the Site SHE Plan and comply with its provisions.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 72,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 73,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 74,\n      \"text\": \"4.7     The details of contents to be covered in the\
      \ site SHE plan are given in Appendix\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 75,\n      \"text\": \"         No.: 3.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 76,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"source_line_number\": 77,\n \

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"3.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"\
      3.0      Compliance\\n\\n3.1     Memorandum of Understanding (MOU)\\n\\n\\n3.1.1   A Memorandum of Understanding\
      \ placed at Appendix No.: 1 shall be executed after\\n          the award of contract before commencement\
      \ of work by the contractor with regard to\\n          various provisions on Safety, Health and Environment\
      \ to be practiced during the\\n          construction work.\\n\\n\\n3.2     BMRC’s SHE Policy and Management\
      \ Systems\\n\\n\\n3.2.1    The construction works shall be undertaken in accordance with BMRC’s SHE Policy\\\
      n        and Management Systems as amended from time to time provided in Project SHE\\n         Manual.\\\
      n\\n\\n3.3      Indian statutory requirements\\n\\n3.3.1     Primary statutory regulations\\n\\n\\n3.3.1.1\
      \  Contractor shall develop thorough understanding about Building and Other\\n        Construction Workers\
      \ (Regulation of Employment and Conditions of Service) Act\\n        1996, Central Rules 1998, the building\
      \ & other construction workers welfare cess\\n        act 1996 and  central welfare rules 1998, Building and\
      \ Other Construction\\n       Workers [RE&C] Karnataka  Rules,  2006,  Building and Other Construction\\n\
      \       Workers Welfare Cess act 1996 and Central Rules 1998, Notification [Central &\\n         State] –\
      \ Collection of Cess, not only to satisfy the Inspectors’ perspective but the\\n       use  of  legislation\
      \  as  the  strong  tool  for  effective SHE management  at\\n        construction worksites. Contractor is\
      \ strongly advised to practice the principle\\n         of voluntary compliance.\\n\\n\\n 3.3.1.2 In order\
      \ to facilitate the contractor for better understanding on the various provisions of\\n         the above\
      \ Act and Rules, a tabulated information highlighting the Sections/Rules\\n          referring to the corresponding\
      \ registration of contractors, maintenance of registers and\\n         records, hours of work and wages, cess\
      \ & welfare, medical  facilities and safety\\n        requirements are given in Appendix No. 2. It is an indicative\
      \ one and not a limiting list.\\n\\n\\n3.3.2   In addition, the construction works shall be undertaken in\
      \ accordance with all applicable\\n         legislation and Indian statutory requirements listed below but\
      \ not limiting to:\\n\\n\\n                i)     Indian Electricity Act 2003 and Rules 1956\\n\\n       \
      \           ii)    National Building Code, 2005\\n\\n                  iii)    Factories Act, 1948, Delhi,\
      \ Uttarpradesh, Haryana Factories Rules.\\n\\n             iv)   Motor Vehicles Act as amended in 1994 and\
      \ The Central Motor Vehicles Rules,\\n              1989.\\n\\n           v)    Indian Road Congress Code\
      \ IRC: SP: 55-2001 ‘Guidelines on Safety In Road\\n               Construction Zones.\\n\\n             vi)\
      \   The Petroleum Act, 1934 and Rules 1976\\n\\n              vii)  Gas Cylinder Rules, 2003\\n\\n       \
      \        viii)   Indian Explosives Act. 1884, along with the Explosives substance Act 1908 and\\n        \
      \        the Explosives Rules 1983\\n\\n             ix)   The (Indian) Boilers Act, 1923\\n\\n          \
      \ x)   The Public Liability Insurance Act 1991 and Rules 1991\\n\\n             xi)   Minimum Wages Act, 1948\
      \ and Rules 1950\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"xii)   Contract Labour Act,\
      \ 1970 and Rules 1971\\n\\n                               xiii)   Child Labour (Prohibitions & Regulations)\
      \ Act, 1986 and Rules 1950\\n\\n                        xiv)  Environment Protection Act, 1986 and Rules 1986\\\
      n\\n                     xv)   Air (Prevention and control of Pollution) Act, 1981\\n\\n                 \
      \       xvi)  Water (Prevention and Control of Pollution) Act, 1974\\n\\n                          xvii) \
      \ The Noise Pollution (Regulation & Control) Rules, 2000\\n\\n                           xviii)  Notification\
      \ on Control of Noise from Diesel Generator (DG) sets, 2002\\n\\n                       xix)  Recycled Plastic\
      \ Usage Rules, 1998\\n\\n                    xx)   Notification, Central Ground Water Board, Act January 1997\\\
      n\\n                       xxi)  Manufacture, Storage & Import of Hazardous Chemicals Rules, 1989\\n\\n  \
      \                        xxii)  The Hazardous Waste (Management & Handling) Rules, 1989\\n\\n            \
      \               xxiii) Hazardous Waste Management Rules 1989 (as amended in 1999)\\n\\n                  \
      \    xxiv) Karnataka Preservation of Trees act, 1976 & rules, Batteries (Management and\\n               \
      \          Handling) Rules\\n\\n                    xxv)  Fly ash utilization notification, Sept 1999 as amended\
      \ in August 2003\\n\\n                      xxvi) Guidelines of Karnataka Urban Arts Commission\\n\\n    \
      \                    xxvii) Mysore Tramway Act.\\n\\n\\n          3.3.3    Workman Compensation Act, 1923\
      \ along with allied Rules\\n\\n\\n           3.3.3.1  The contractor shall ensure that all his employees /\
      \ workmen are covered under\\n               ‘Workmen Compensation Act’ and shall pay compensation to his\
      \ workmen as\\n               and when the eventuality for the same arises.\\n\\n\\n          3.3.4     Notwithstanding\
      \ the above Act/Rules, there  is nothing  in those  to exempt the\\n                     contractor from the\
      \ purview of any other Act or Rule in Republic of India for the safety\\n                      of men and\
      \ materials.\\n\\n\\n          3.3.5        If the requirements stated in this document are less stringent\
      \ than or in conflict with\\n                    the country’s applicable legislation, the latter shall apply.\\\
      n\\n\\n          3.4       International Standards, Guidelines & ISO Certifications\\n\\n\\n          3.4.1\
      \    The works should be undertaken in accordance with the applicable international\\n                   \
      \  guidelines, standards and specifications on SHE and every contract shall aim to\\n                   achieve\
      \ ISO certifications listed below during the currency of the contract:\\n\\n\\n             OHSAS 18001-1999\
      \   : Occupational Health and Safety Management System.\\n\\n                ISO 14001-2004         : Environmental\
      \ Management Systems.\\n\\n          3.4.2    The process of certification shall start immediately after the\
      \ award of the work and\\n                  complete within reasonable time. Towards this, the contractor\
      \ shall undertake the\\n                    required steps including appointment of ISO consultant for obtaining\
      \ the certification\\n                on  Occupational  Health  and  Safety  Management  System  and  Environment\\\
      n                Management System.\\n\\n\\n          3.4.3     In case of failure on the part of the contractor,\
      \ the Employer at the cost of the\\n                  contractor shall do the same.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\
