>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 30,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 31,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 32,
>       "text": "                i)    The Contractor shall maintain the confidential records of medical examination or"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 33,
>       "text": "                the physician authorized by the Employer."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 34,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 35,
>       "text": "                  ii)   No building or other construction worker is charged for the medical examination"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 36,
>       "text": "             and the cost of such examination is borne by contractor employing such building"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 37,
>       "text": "               worker."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 38,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 39,
>       "text": "                  iii)   The medical examination shall include: -"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 40,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 41,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 42,
>       "text": "                a)     Full medical and occupational history."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 43,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 44,
>       "text": "                b)     Clinical examination with particular reference to"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 45,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 46,
>       "text": "                                    i)    General Physique;"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 47,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 48,
>       "text": "                                       ii)     Vision:- Total visual performance using standard orthorator  like"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 49,
>       "text": "                         Titmus  Vision  Tester  should be  estimated and  suitability  for"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 50,
>       "text": "                         placement  ascertained  in accordance  with  the  prescribed  job"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 51,
>       "text": "                           standards."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 52,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 53,
>       "text": "                                        iii)    Hearing:  - Persons with normal must be able to hear a forced"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 54,
>       "text": "                          whisper at twenty-four feet. Persons using hearing aids must be"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 55,
>       "text": "                           able to hear a warning shout under noisy working conditions."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 56,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 57,
>       "text": "                             iv)    Breathing:- Peak flow rate using standard peak flow meter and the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 58,
>       "text": "                        average peak flow rate determined out of these readings of the test"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 59,
>       "text": "                          performed.  The  results  recorded  at  pre-placement  medical"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 60,
>       "text": "                          examination could be used as a standard for the same individual at"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 61,
>       "text": "                           the same altitude for reference during subsequent examination."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 62,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 63,
>       "text": "                        v)   Upper Limbs: - Adequate arm function and grip"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 64,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 65,
>       "text": "                             vi)    Spine: - Adequately flexible for the job concerned."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 66,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 67,
>       "text": "                               vii)   Lower Limbs: - Adequate leg and foot concerned."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 68,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 69,
>       "text": "                                 viii)   General: - Mental alertness and stability with good eye, hand and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 70,
>       "text": "                              foot coordination."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 71,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 72,
>       "text": "                 c)   Any other tests which the examining doctor considers necessary"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 73,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 74,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 75,
>       "text": "42.1.2.     If the contractor fails to get the medical examination conducted as mentioned above,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 76,
>       "text": "          the employer will have the right to get the same conducted by through an agency with"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 77,
>       "text": "           intimation to the contractor and deduct the cost and overhead charges."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 78,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 79,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 80,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 81,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 82,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 3,
>       "text": "          42.2     Occupational Health Centre"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 4,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 5,
>       "text": "          42.2.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site an occupational health centre,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 6,
>       "text": "                 mobile or static is provided and maintained in good order. Services and facilities"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 7,
>       "text": "                as per the scale lay down in Schedule X of BOCWR. A construction medical"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 8,
>       "text": "                     officer appointed in an occupational health centre, possess the qualification as"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 9,
>       "text": "                     laid down in Schedule XI of BOCWR."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 10,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 11,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 12,
>       "text": "          42.3    Ambulance van and room"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 13,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 14,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 15,
>       "text": "          42.3.1   The contractor  shall ensure  at a construction  site of a building or other"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 16,
>       "text": "                  construction work that an ambulance van and room are provided at such"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 17,
>       "text": "                  construction site or an arrangement is made with a nearby hospital for providing"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 18,
>       "text": "                such ambulance van for transportation of serious cases of accident or sickness"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 19,
>       "text": "                   of workers  to  hospital promptly and such ambulance van and room are"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 20,
>       "text": "                 maintained in good repair and is equipped with standard facilities specified in"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 21,
>       "text": "                Schedule IV and Schedule V of BOCWR."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 22,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 23,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 24,
>       "text": "          42.4      First-aid boxes"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 25,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 26,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 27,
>       "text": "          42.4.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site one First-aid box for 100 workers"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 28,
>       "text": "                   provided and maintained for providing First-aid to the building workers. Every First-aid"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 29,
>       "text": "                 box  is  distinctly marked  “First-aid” and  is equipped with the  articles specified  in"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 30,
>       "text": "                 Schedule III of BOCWR."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 31,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 32,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 33,
>       "text": "          42.5     HIV/ AIDS prevention and control"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 34,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 35,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 36,
>       "text": "          42.5.1   The contractor shall adopt the Employer’s Policy on “HIV  / AIDS Prevention and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 37,
>       "text": "                    Control for Workmen Engaged by Contractors” and the copy of the policy is given in"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 38,
>       "text": "                Appendix No.: 4."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 39,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 40,
>       "text": "          42.5.2   The Employer will engage a professional agency for implementing the guidelines laid"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 41,
>       "text": "               down in the policy and communicate to the contractor."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 42,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 43,
>       "text": "          42.5.3   The Contractor shall extend necessary support to the appointed agency by deputing"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 44,
>       "text": "                    the workmen to attend the awareness creation programmes."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 45,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 46,
>       "text": "          42.5.4   The contractor shall also extend necessary organizational support to the appointed"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 47,
>       "text": "                 agency  for the  effective implementation  of the Employers’ workplace  policy on"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 48,
>       "text": "                 HIV/AIDS for workmen of the Contractors."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 49,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 50,
>       "text": "          42.5.5   As laid down in the policy the contractor shall identify peer educators (1 for every 100"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 51,
>       "text": "                   workers) and refer them for professional training to the Employers’ appointed agency"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 52,
>       "text": "                       for the purpose."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 53,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 54,
>       "text": "          42.5.6   The peer educators on completion of the training shall serve as the focal point"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 55,
>       "text": "                    for any information, education and awareness campaign among the workmen"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 56,
>       "text": "                 throughout the contract period."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 57,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 58,
>       "text": "          42.5.7   The peer educators will be paid a monthly honorarium as fixed by the Employer for"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 59,
>       "text": "                   rendering his services in addition to his regular duty."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 60,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 61,
>       "text": "          42.5.8   The total number of peer educators (1 for 100 workers) shall always be maintained by"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 62,
>       "text": "                    the contractor."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 63,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 64,
>       "text": "          42.5.9    In case if these peer educators leave the contractor by creating vacancy, then"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 65,
>       "text": "                  the contractor at his own expense train the new replacement peer educator from"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 66,
>       "text": "                  the Employers’ appointed agency for the purpose."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 67,
>       "text": "          42.5.10    It is suggested to the contractor that due care should be taken to select the peer"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 68,
>       "text": "                   educators from among the group of workmen so that they remain with the contractor"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 69,
>       "text": "                   throughout the contract period."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 70,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 71,
>       "text": "          42.6     Prevention of mosquito breeding"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 72,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 73,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 74,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 75,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 3,
>       "text": "42.6.1   Measures shall be taken to prevent mosquito breeding at site. The measures to be"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 4,
>       "text": "         taken shall include:"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 5,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 6,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 7,
>       "text": "                i)    Empty cans, oil drums, packing and other receptacles, which may retain water"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 8,
>       "text": "                  shall be deposited at a central collection point and shall be removed from the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 9,
>       "text": "                   site regularly."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 10,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 11,
>       "text": "                  ii)      Still waters shall be treated at least once every week with oil in order to prevent"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 12,
>       "text": "              mosquito breeding."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 13,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 14,
>       "text": "                  iii)    Contractor’s equipment and other items on the site, which may retain water,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 15,
>       "text": "                  shall be stored, covered or treated in such a manner that water could not be"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 16,
>       "text": "                 retained."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 17,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 18,
>       "text": "             iv)   Water storage tanks shall be provided."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 19,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 20,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 21,
>       "text": "42.6.2    Posters in both Hindi, English and local language which draw attention to the dangers"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 22,
>       "text": "           of permitting mosquito breeding, shall be displayed prominently on the site."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 23,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 24,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 25,
>       "text": "42.6.3   The contractor at periodic interval shall arrange to prevent mosquito breeding"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 26,
>       "text": "        by fumigation  / spraying of insecticides. Most  effective insecticides  shall"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 27,
>       "text": "         include SOLFAC WP 10 or Baytex, The Ideal Larvicide etc."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 28,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 29,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 30,
>       "text": "42.7     Alcohol and drugs"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 31,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 32,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 33,
>       "text": "42.7.1   The contractor shall ensure at  all times that no employee  is working under the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 34,
>       "text": "          influence of alcohol / drugs which are punishable under Govt. regulations."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 35,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 36,

### J96c0758e1616249f

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part3#Clause 43.0 — Noise|Clause 43.0 — Noise]]

> {
>   "pdf_page": 66,
>   "text": "43.0     Noise\n\n\n43.1    The Contractor shall consider noise as an environmental constraint in his design,\n          planning and execution of the Works and provide demonstrable evidence of the same\n        on Employer’s request. The Contractor shall, at his own expense, take all appropriate\n        measures  to ensure  that work  carried out by the Contractor and by  his sub-\n          Contractors, whether on or off the Site, will not cause any unnecessary or excessive\n         noise which may disturb the occupants of any nearby dwellings, schools, hospitals, or\n         premises with similar sensitivity to noise.\n\n\n43.1.1    Without prejudice to the generality of the foregoing, noise level reduction measures\n           shall include the following:\n\n\n                i)    The Contractor shall ensure that all powered mechanical equipment used in the\n             Works shall be effectively sound reduced using the most modern techniques\n                available including but not limited to silencers and mufflers.\n\n                  ii)   The Contractor shall construct acoustic screens or enclosures around any parts\n                 of the Works from which excessive noise may be generated.\n\n\n43.1.2   The Contractor shall ensure that noise generated by work carried out by the Contractor\n        and his sub-Contractors during daytime and night time shall not exceed the maximum\n          permissible noise limits, whether continuously or intermittently, as given in the project\n       SHE Manual. The same may be varied from time to time by and at the sole discretion\n           of the Employer, In the event of a breach of this requirement, the Contractor shall\n         immediately re-deploy or adjust the relevant equipment or take other appropriate\n        measures to reduce the noise levels and thereafter maintain them at levels which do\n          not exceed the said limits. Such measures may include without limitation the temporary\n          or permanent cessation of use of certain items of equipment."
> }

>   "text": "Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\n255\n          44.1.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site of a building or other construction\n                 work that all working areas in a free tunnel are provided with ventilation system as\n                 approved by the DG and the fresh air supply in such tunnel is not less than 6m3/min\n                       for each building worker employed underground in such tunnel and the free air flow\n                movement inside such tunnel is not less than 9m/min.\n\n\n          44.1.2   The oxygen level shall not be less than 19.5% in the working environment.\n\n\n          44.2      Illumination\n\n          44.2.1   The contractor shall take every effort to illuminate the work site as per the Employer’s\n                   requirement illustrated in general instruction BMRC/SHE/CEO/011.\n\n          44.2.2   The contractor shall conduct a monthly illumination monitoring by lux meter for\n                         all the locations and the report shall be sent to the Employer within 7th of the\n                  next month and the same shall be reviewed during the monthly SHE committee\n                  meeting.\n\n\n          45.0     Radiation\n\n\n          45.1    The use of radioactive substances and radiating apparatus shall comply with the Govt.\n                    regulatory requirements and all subsidiary legislation\n\n\n          45.2     Operations involving ionising radiation shall only be carried out after having been\n                  reviewed without objection by the Employers representative and shall be carried out in\n                  accordance with a method statement.\n\n\n          45.3     each area containing irradiated apparatus shall have warning notices and barriers, as\n                    required by the Regulations, conspicuously posted at or near the area.\n\n\n          45.4     Radioactive substances will be stored, used or disposed shall be strictly in accordance\n                     with the Govt. Enactments.\n\n\n          45.5    The contractor shall ensure that all site personnel and members of the public are not\n                 exposed to radiation.\n\n\n          46.0     Welfare measures for workers\n\n\n          46.1      Latrine and Urinal Accommodation\n\n\n          46.1.1   The contractor shall provide one latrine seat for every 20 workers up to 100\n                 workers and thereafter one for every additional 50 workers. In addition one\n                    urinal accommodation shall be provided for every 100 workers.\n\n\n          46.1.2   When women are employed, separate latrine and urinals accommodation shall be\n                   provided on the same scale as mentioned above.\n\n\n          46.1.3    Latrine and urinals shall be provided as per Section 33 of BOCWA and maintained as\n                   per Rule 243 of BOCWR and shall also comply with the requirements of public health\n                      authorities\n\n\n          46.1.4   Moving sites\n\n\n          46.1.4.1  In case of works like track laying, the zone of work is constantly moving at elevated\n                      level or at underground level. In such cases mobile toilets with proper facility to drain\n                    the sullage shall be provided at reasonably accessible distance.\n\n\n          46.1.5    In case if the contractor fail to provide required number of urinals and latrines\n                  or fail to maintain it as per the requirements of Public Health laws, the Employer\n\n\n\n         Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 67 of 114",

### S10ec60af02b3e977

[[OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ#1.0 — Pricing Document Preamble and Instructions|1.0 — Pricing Document Preamble and Instructions]]

> - **Read BOQ with the Complete Tender and Contract Documents** (Instruction 1)
> - **Provisional Quantities and Payment for Measured Work** (Instruction 2)
> - **Price Bid Preparation in the Prescribed XLS Sheet** (Instruction 3; ITT 10)
>   - Schedule-A: percentage rate to two decimals; At Par / Excess / Less
>   - Schedule-B: At Par only
>   - Schedule-C: lump-sum salvage credit to BMRCL
> - **Proportionate Recovery and Adjustment of Salvage Credit** (Instruction 4)
> - **Salvage Credit Based on Quoted Price** (Instruction 5)
> - **Taxes and Statutory Levies Included, Except GST** (Instruction 6)
> - **Measurement of Completed Work** (Instruction 7)
> - **Completed-Work Pricing and Included Execution Costs** (Instruction 8)
> - **Included Safety and Protection Costs** (Instruction 9)
> - **Overheads, Profit, Risks and Defect-Remedy Obligations** (Instruction 10)
> - **Accommodation, Insurance, Wages and Approval Costs** (Instruction 11)
> 
> 
> 
> - **Execution Standards and Engineer’s Directions** (Instruction 12)
> - **Incidental Removal Works Without Separate Payment** (Instruction 13)
> - **Temporary Relocation and Protection of Utilities** (Instruction 14)
> - **Temporary Works, Power and Supports at Contractor’s Cost** (Instruction 15)
> - **Permanent Supports and Restoration Payment Provisions** (Instruction 16)
> - **Balcony and Canopy Area at 50%; Chejjas Excluded from Payment** (Instruction 17)
> - **Demolition Depth and Refilling of Low-Level Areas** (Instruction 18)
> - **Removal Within the Line of Cut, Excluding Trees** (Instruction 19)
> - **Total Floor Area as the Sum of Built Areas of All Floors** (Instruction 20)
> - **Building Additions or Deletions and Accepted Percentage Rates** (Instruction 21)

### Jf17d55c6acfde864

[[OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ#Financial Document and Bill of Quantities|Financial Document and Bill of Quantities]]

> {
>   "page_type": "text",
>   "pdf_page": 6,
>   "review_status": "pending_manual_review",
>   "text": " BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial Document \n \n \n \nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026 \nSection Page 3 of 7 \n \nPricing Document-Preamble \n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ) & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract Document including \nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’), \nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \nEmployer’s Requirements, Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \nCorrigendum and Addendums. \n2. The quantities given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \n(provisional). The basis of payment will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified by the Engineer and valued at \nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”. \n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \nclause 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A, for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit \nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \n4. The Lump sum amount indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \naccount bills proportionately. In case of any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased according to the value of work \ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \n5. The Contractor should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \nreference to the quoted price and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the \nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \n7. The method of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \nthe requirements as stated in the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the tender documents. \n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all respects \ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \nconstructional plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \ntransportation, disposal of debris at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts, all temporary works & false \nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection facilities. \n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/ \ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \nprotection of adjoining land & building while working. \n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead charges, \ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities \nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \nduring the Defect Liability Period.  \n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff & workmen, \nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \n305\n",

### J002388868106a238

[[OKF_bundle/Financial/vol4_secJ#Financial Document and Bill of Quantities|Financial Document and Bill of Quantities]]

> {
>   "page_type": "text",
>   "pdf_page": 7,
>   "review_status": "pending_manual_review",
>   "text": " BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial Document \n \n \n \nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026 \nSection Page 4 of 7 \n \n12. The work is to be executed as per Bill of Quantity description, Technical Specifications, \nspecifications of MORTH, KPWD, CPWD, drawings, best engineering practices and/or \nas directed by the Engineer.  \n13. Removal of hoardings, interior lighting and interior furniture etc., ACP cladding and \nstructural glazing etc., which are existing within the line of cut are incidental to work.  No \nseparate payment will be considered for payment. \n14. If it is necessary to maintain any power, water or other utilities during demolition, such \nlines shall be temporarily relocated, as necessary and protected before commencement \nof demolition work.   \n15. All such cost involved for temporary relocation/maintenance must be borne by contractor \nand no payment will be made for temporary works. The contractor shall make his own \narrangements for power requirement for his equipment/machinery. If advised by the \nConsultant/BMRCL, the contractor has to give temporary supports for the building during \ndemolition wherever necessary without any extra financial implications to BMRCL. \n16. If advised by the Consultant/BMRCL, the contractor has to give permanent supports like \nwall, columns (Steel, RCC, etc.), including plastering, painting, plumbing, water supply \nand sanitary works, electrical restoration works, etc. to the supported portion of the \nbuilding before or after total/partial demolition, wherever necessary to maintain LOC, for \nwhich payment will be made as per  accepted rate in BoQ schedule for items available in \nCommon SR for the year 2023-24 (Schedule of Rates),  for the execution of such work.  \n17. 50% area of Balcony and Canopy if exists will be considered for calculating area for \npayment as per the guide lines for valuation of properties. The area of chejjas or such \nsimilar coverings/shadows will not be considered for payment.  \n18. The demolition and dismantling shall be carried out up to ground and/or up to basement \nlevel as the case may be including underground water storage structures if any. Refilling \nthe low-level areas up to ground level is within the scope of contractor’s work.  \n19. The items existing excluding trees within the proposed line of cut have to be dismantled \nand demolished and debris to be transported and disposed. \n20. The total floor area means the summation of total built area of all floors in the buildings \nindicated in Schedule A. \n21. In case some buildings are deleted or added in BOQ, the rates per sqm for that \ndeletion/addition will be same as given above. For payment purpose, the %age quoted \nand accepted in the Summary Cost will be used for the above rate.  \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n306\n",

>   "text": " BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial Document \n \n \n \nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026 \nSection Page 2 of 7 \n \nPRICING DOCUMENT - LETTER OF PRICE BID \n(To be submitted in the letter head of the Tenderer) \n \nRef: \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n \n              Dated………. \n(Tenderer to provide date and reference no.) \n To \n The Managing Director,  \n Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd. \n Fourth floor, BMTC Complex \n KH Road, Shanthinagar \n Bangalore-560027, Karnataka,  \n India. \n \nDear Sir, \nPriced document for ……………………… (Name of Work) and …………….. (Tender No.). \n \n \n1. We, hereby submit our priced document for the above-mentioned work.  \n \n2. We understand that in the event of any information furnished by us being found later on \nto be incorrect or any material information having been suppressed, the following action \ncan be taken in addition to the Provisions of Clause 4.33 of General Conditions of \nContract (GCC).  \n \n(i) Any tender submitted by us in future for other works on the basis of qualification may \nnot be considered. \n \n(ii) If any tender from us is accepted and a contract awarded to us on the basis of our \nqualification, the tender acceptance may be withdrawn and the contract awarded to \nus may be cancelled without any financial claim /Arbitration request from our side. \n \n(iii) Our name will / may be removed from the panel of qualified agencies. \n \n \n \nYours faithfully, \n(Signature) …………………… \nName of signatory: ……………………………… \nCapacity of signatory: …………………………… \nName and address of Tenderer:……………….. \n \n \n \n \n304\n",

Section -B      Instructions to Tenderers (ITT)\n                                 Section -C       Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria (EQC)\n                                 Section -D      Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)\n\n                                             General Conditions of Contract (GCC) including\n                                 Section -E\n                                                Contract Data\n                                 Section -F      Special Conditions of Contract (SCC)\n                                 Section -G     Contract Forms\n\n                                            Technical Document (Volume -2)\n\n                                Section-H     Employer’s Requirements & Technical Specifications\n\n                                            Technical Document (Volume -3)\n                                    Section-I       Safety, Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\n\n                                      Financial Document (Pricing Document) (Volume -4)\n\n                                                      Letter of Price Bid\n\n                                           Preamble\n                                 Section -J\n                                                                     Bill of Quantities (BOQ)\n\n                                     Summary of Bill of Quantities (.xls)\n\n\n\n         5.2      The Tenderer is expected to examine carefully all instructions, forms, terms, conditions,\n                      specifications, and other contents of the Tender Documents, and to take them fully into\n                   account before submitting the Tender. ### Jecec2d2b1a0fba3a

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 20.0 — Clarification of Tenders|Clause 20.0 — Clarification of Tenders]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "20.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 20,
>       "text": "20.0     CLARIFICATION OF TENDERS\n\n         20.1       Evaluation of Technical Bids submitted by Tenderers shall be undertaken based on details\n                   submitted in the Technical Bids only. Employer may at his discretion ask any Tenderer for\n                        clarification on his submittals through eProcurement portal. The Tenderer, in response to the\n                         clarification, is not permitted to submit additional document not pertaining to the clarification\n                    sought. The request for clarification and the response shall be in writing only through\n                  eProcurement Portal and no response leading to material change in the submittals will be\n                   taken into consideration.  It is not mandatory for Employer to seek clarification. Employer\n                   reserves the right to evaluate the bids based on documents submitted by the bidders.\n\n         20.2       Notwithstanding the provision in Para 20.1 above, the Tenderer must take care that all\n                    required documents  to  establish  their  Eligibility &  Qualification and other mandatory\n                   requirements must be submitted before the deadline for submission.\n\n         20.3      During the evaluation of financial bid, the Employer may ask Tenderers individually for\n                        clarification regarding their price bids, including the breakup of prices. The request for\n                        clarification and the response shall be in writing and no change in the price or substance of\n                    the Price bid shall be sought, offered, or permitted except as required to confirm the correction\n                      of arithmetical errors discovered by the Employer during the evaluation of Tenders in\n                  accordance with Clause 22.2.5.\n\n         20.4           If a Bidder does not provide clarifications of his bid by the date and time set in the Employer’s\n                    request for clarification, their bid shall be evaluated as per the available information in the\n                   submitted bid."
>     }
>   ],
>   "heading_line": "20.0     CLARIFICATION OF TENDERS",
>   "review_status": "pending_manual_review"
> }

>       "text": "1      DEFINITIONS AND INTERPRETATION\n\n       Definitions    1.1       The following contract terms and expressions as used in the contract defined shall\n                             have the meanings assigned to them, except where the context otherwise requires.\n                           Words indicating persons or parties include corporations and other legal entities\n                                except where the context requires otherwise.\n\n                        1.1.1     Documents\n\n                        1.1.1.1    “Appendix/Attachment”  means  the  completed  pages  in   title  Appendix/\n                                 Attachment, which are appended to and form part of the Tender.\n                        1.1.1.2     “Construction  and/or  Manufacture  Documents”  means   all  drawings,\n                                     calculations, computer software (programs), samples, patterns, models, operation\n                             and maintenance manuals, and other manuals and information of a similar nature,\n                                      to be submitted by the Contractor.\n                        1.1.1.3     “Contract” means the written Contract Agreement, the Letter of Acceptance,\n                               General Conditions of Contract, Special Conditions of Contract, the Employer’s\n                              Requirements& Technical specifications, the Tender, the Notice of Invitation to\n                                Tender, Instructions to Tenderers, the Contractor’s Proposal, the Schedules of\n                         BOQ, Clarifications issued and such further documents which are listed in the Letter\n                                      of Acceptance or Contract Agreement (if completed).\n                        1.1.1.4     “Contract Agreement” means the contract agreement pursuant to acceptance\n                                           letter  referred  to  in Sub-Clause  1.4.   It  shall  also  include  all subsequent\n                                    modifications/  amendments  to  record  the  Contract  as  a  result  of  the\n                               communications or negotiation proceedings between the parties.\n                        1.1.1.5     “Contractor’s Proposal” means the proposal submitted by the Contractor with the\n                                Tender, as modified and accepted by the Employer and included in the Contract.\n                            Such documents may include the Contractor’s preliminary Design.\n                        1.1.1.6    “Design Data” means all specifications, plans, drawings, details, graphs, sketches,\n                                models, levels, setting-out dimensions, calculations duly checked by the Contractor\n                             and other documents relating to the Design of the works prepared or to be prepared\n                              by or on behalf of the drawings as any, from time to time, be\n                                   furnished or for which the Engineer has issued a Notice of No Objection.\n                        1.1.1.8    “Employer’s             applicable) as included in the Contract, and any alterations and modifications\n                                    thereto in accordance with the Contract.\n                        1.1.1.9     “Letter of Acceptance” means the letter from the Employer or the Engineer, or a\n                               person nominated by them on their behalf for this purpose, to the                    writing by the Employer and the Contractor prior to acceptance of the Tender.\n                       1.1.1.15   “Works Programme” means the programme showing the sequence, method and\n                                    timing of investigations, Design, issue of No Objection Notices, execution (including\n                                     dismantling/demolition/restoration),  manufacture,  delivery  to   site,  erection,\n                                         installation, testing, commissioning of the Works, indigenisation (where provided that the\n                                      definition shall exclude the Contractor and his sub-contractors of any tier in\n                                     relation to the Works.\n                 1.1.2.5    “Designer” means the Contractor, or part of the group forming the contractor,\n                           person, firm or company or group of companies, or any replacement, carrying out\n                           the Design or part thereof.\n                 1.1.2.6    “Employer” means BANGALORE METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED\n                       (BMRCL), its legal successors and assignees.\n                 1.1.2.7    “Engineer” means Chief Engineer or Addl. Chief Engineer or Deputy Chief\n                         Engineer or General Consultant or any person/party nominated or appointed from\n                           time to time by the Employer to act as the Engineer for the purposes of the Contract\n                       and notified as such in writing to the Contractor.\n                 1.1.2.8    “General Consultant” or “GC” means the firm/JV or consortium of firms appointed\n                        by the Employer as a general consultant to the Employer or any other person\n                          appointed from time to time and notified as such to the Contractor.\n                 1.1.2.9     “Sub-Contractor” means  the  Individual,  Firm, Company,  Corporation,  Joint\n                         Venture or Consortium, having direct contract with the Contractor and to whom any\n                             part of the Work has been sublet by the Contractor, with prior permission of the\n                         Engineer or Employer and shall include his heirs, his executors, administrators,\n                          successors, legal representatives, as the case may be.\n                1.1.2.10   “Partner/member” means partner or member of  joint venture, as the context\n                             requires.\n                1.1.2.11    “Tenderer” or “Bidder” means the person, firm or corporation, Joint Venture\n                            submitting a tender against the notice of invitation of tender and shall include his\n                               heirs, his executors, administrators, successors, legal representatives, as the case\n                    may be. Tenderer shall mean ‘Contractor’ wherever the context so requires and\n                             vice-versa.\n                1.1.3      Dates, Times and Periods\n\n                 1.1.3.1    “Commencement Date  “will be reckoned as the date of signing of Contract\n                        Agreement.\n                 1.1.3.2     “Contract Period” means the period from the Commencement Date to the end of\n                                  final Defects Liability Period.\n                 1.1.3.3    “Day” means a calendar day; “Week” means 7 calendar days; “Month” means\n                         Georgian calendar month and “Year” means 365 calendar days.\n                 1.1.3.4     “Gazetted Holiday” means every holiday which is observed by Bangalore Metro\n                             Rail Corporation Limited as a gazetted holiday.\n                 1.1.3.5    “General Holiday” means Sunday.\n                 1.1.3.6    “Key Date  / Mile Stones” means a date identified as such in the Contract\n                       documents as the same may be extended by the Engineer pursuant to GCC clause\n                              8.4.\n                 1.1.3.7    “Stage” means level of progress of the works identified as such and more\n                               particularly described in the Employer’s Requirements for which a Key Date for the\n                        achievement thereof is stipulated in the Contract.\n                 1.1.3.8    “Time for Completion” means the time for completing the Works physically or a\n                            section or a part thereof (as the case may be), and passing the Tests on Completion\n                             including  Integrated  Testing  and Commissioning, as stated in the Contract and\n                        as applicable for the nature of contract calculated from the Commencement Date."
